Process each trace\n    for tr in st:\n        try:\n            # Get trace metadata\n            stats = tr.stats\n            station_id = f\"{stats.network}.{stats.station}\"\n\n            # Prepare waveform data for the model\n            # EQTransformer expects 3-component data or single component\n            trace_data = tr.data\n            sampling_rate = stats.sampling_rate\n\n            # Normalize the data\n            if len(trace_data) > 0:\n                max_val = np.max(np.abs(trace_data))\n                if max_val > 0:\n                    trace_data = trace_data / max_val\n\n                # The model expects specific trace formatting\n                # We need to pass it as a list of traces with metadata\n                trace_dict = {\n                    'station_id': station_id,\n                    'data': trace_data,\n                    'sampling_rate': sampling_rate,\n                    'starttime': stats.starttime\n                }\n\n                # Apply the model to pick phases\n                # Note: EQTransformer returns picks with probabilities\n                try:\n                    result = model.annotate(trace_data, sampling_rate)\n\n                    # Extract P and S picks from the result\n                    # The exact format depends on the model output\n                    # We'll use a threshold-based approach\n                    if hasattr(result, 'predictions'):\n                        pred = result.predictions\n\n                        # Find peaks in P and S probability curves\n                        # This is simplified - real implementation would be more complex\n                        p_probs = pred[0] if len(pred) > 0 else np.zeros(len(trace_data))\n                        s_probs = pred[1] if len(pred) > 1 else np.zeros(len(trace_data))\n\n                        # Find P picks\n                        p_threshold = 0.3\n                        p_peaks = find_peaks_above_threshold(p_probs, p_threshold)\n\n                        # Find S picks\n                        s_threshold = 0.3\n                        s_peaks = find_peaks_above_threshold(s_probs, s_threshold)\n\n                        # Convert picks to absolute times\n                        for peak_idx in p_peaks:\n                            time_rel = peak_idx / sampling_rate\n                            pick_time = stats.starttime + time_rel\n                            picks.append({\n                                'station_id': station_id,\n                                'phase_type': 'P',\n                                'time': pick_time,\n                                'probability': p_probs[peak_idx]\n                            })\n\n                        for peak_idx in s_peaks:\n                            time_rel = peak_idx / sampling_rate\n                            pick_time = stats.starttime + time_rel\n                            picks.append({\n                                'station_id': station_id,\n                                'phase_type': 'S',\n                                'time': pick_time,\n                                'probability': s_probs[peak_idx]\n                            })\n\n                except Exception as e:\n                    print(f\"Warning: Could not process trace {station_id}: {e}\")\n                    continue\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Warning: Error processing trace: {e}\")\n            continue\n\n    print(f\"Found {len(picks)} phase picks\")\n    return picks\n\ndef find_peaks_above_threshold(prob_array, threshold):\n    \"\"\"Find peaks in probability array above threshold.\"\"\"\n    peaks = []\n    n = len(prob_array)\n\n    if n < 5:\n        return peaks\n\n    # Simple peak finding\n    for i in range(2, n - 2):\n        if prob_array[i] > threshold:\n            # Check if it's a local maximum\n            if (prob_array[i] > prob_array[i-1] and\n                prob_array[i] > prob_array[i+1] and\n                prob_array[i] > prob_array[i-2] and\n                prob_array[i] > prob_array[i+2]):\n                peaks.append(i)\n\n    return peaks\n\ndef associate_phases(picks, station_dict):\n    \"\"\"Associate P and S picks to identify earthquake events.\"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nAssociating phase picks to identify events...\")\n\n    # Convert picks to a more workable format\n    events = []\n\n    # Group picks by time windows\n    # Sort all picks by time\n    picks_sorted = sorted(picks, key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    # Sliding window approach to find events\n    time_window = 30.0  # seconds - look for phases within this window\n\n    for i, pick in enumerate(picks_sorted):\n        # Start a new event if this pick is far from the previous ones\n        if not events or (pick['time'] - events[-1]['latest_time']).total_seconds() > time_window:\n            # Start new event\n            event_picks = [pick]\n            event_start_time = pick['time']\n            event_latest_time = pick['time']\n        else:\n            # Add to existing event\n            event_picks = events[-1]['picks']\n            event_picks.append(pick)\n            events[-1]['picks'] = event_picks\n\n            # Load a pre-trained model for phase picking\n    # Using EQTransformer model which is good for phase picking\n    model = sbmodels.EQTransformer()\n\n    # Prepare data for the model\n    # The model expects specific formatting\n    picks = []\n\n    # Process each trace\n    for tr in st:\n        try:\n            # Get trace metadata\n            stats = tr.stats\n            station_id = f\"{stats.network}.{stats.station}\"\n\n            # Prepare waveform data for the model\n            # EQTransformer expects 3-component data or single component\n            trace_data = tr.data\n            sampling_rate = stats.sampling_rate\n\n            # Normalize the data\n            if len(trace_data) > 0:\n                max_val = np.max(np.abs(trace_data))\n                if max_val > 0:\n                    trace_data = trace_data / max_val\n\n                # The model expects specific trace formatting\n                # We need to pass it as a list of traces with metadata\n                trace_dict = {\n                    'station_id': station_id,\n                    'data': trace_data,\n                    'sampling_rate': sampling_rate,\n                    'starttime': stats.starttime\n                }\n\n                # Apply the model to pick phases\n                # Note: EQTransformer returns picks with probabilities\n                try:\n                    result = model.annotate(trace_data, sampling_rate)\n\n                    # Extract P and S picks from the result\n                    # The exact format depends on the model output\n                    # We'll use a threshold-based approach\n                    if hasattr(result, 'predictions'):\n                        pred = result.predictions\n\n                        # Find peaks in P and S probability curves\n                        # This is simplified - real implementation would be more complex\n                        p_probs = pred[0] if len(pred) > 0 else np.zeros(len(trace_data))\n                        s_probs = pred[1] if len(pred) > 1 else np.zeros(len(trace_data))\n\n                        # Find P picks\n                        p_threshold = 0.3\n                        p_peaks = find_peaks_above_threshold(p_probs, p_threshold)\n\n                        # Find S picks\n                        s_threshold = 0.3\n                        s_peaks = find_peaks_above_threshold(s_probs, s_threshold)\n\n                        # Convert picks to absolute times\n                        for peak_idx in p_peaks:\n                            time_rel = peak_idx / sampling_rate\n                            pick_time = stats.starttime + time_rel\n                            picks.append({\n                                'station_id': station_id,\n                                'phase_type': 'P',\n                                'time': pick_time,\n                                'probability': p_probs[peak_idx]\n                            })\n\n                        for peak_idx in s_peaks:\n                            time_rel = peak_idx / sampling_rate\n                            pick_time = stats.starttime + time_rel\n                            picks.append({\n                                'station_id': station_id,\n                                'phase_type': 'S',\n                                'time': pick_time,\n                                'probability': s_probs[peak_idx]\n                            })\n\n                except Exception as e:\n                    print(f\"Warning: Could not process trace {station_id}: {e}\")\n                    continue\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Warning: Error processing trace: {e}\")\n            continue\n\n    print(f\"Found {len(picks)} phase picks\")\n    return picks\n\ndef find_peaks_above_threshold(prob_array, threshold):\n    \"\"\"Find peaks in probability array above threshold.\"\"\"\n    peaks = []\n    n = len(prob_array)\n\n    if n < 5:\n        return peaks\n\n    # Simple peak finding\n    for i in range(2, n - 2):\n        if prob_array[i] > threshold:\n            # Check if it's a local maximum\n            if (prob_array[i] > prob_array[i-1] and\n                prob_array[i] > prob_array[i+1] and\n                prob_array[i] > prob_array[i-2] and\n                prob_array[i] > prob_array[i+2]):\n                peaks.append(i)\n\n    return peaks\n\ndef associate_phases(picks, station_dict):\n    \"\"\"Associate P and S picks to identify earthquake events.\"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nAssociating phase picks to identify events...\")\n\n    # Convert picks to a more workable format\n    events = []\n\n    # Group picks by time windows\n    # Sort all picks by time\n    picks_sorted = sorted(picks, key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    # Sliding window approach to find events\n    time_window = 30.0  # seconds - look for phases within this window\n\n    for i, pick in enumerate(picks_sorted):\n        # Start a new event if this pick is far from the previous ones\n        if not events or (pick['time'] - events[-1]['latest_time']).total_seconds() > time_window:\n            # Start new event\n            event_picks = [pick]\n            event_start_time = pick['time']\n            event_latest_time = pick['time']\n        else:\n            # Add to existing event\n            event_picks = events[-1]['picks']\n            event_picks.append(pick)\n            events[-1]['picks'] = event_picks\n\n            # Update latest time\n            if pick['time'] > event_latest_time:\n                event_latest_time = pick['time']\n\n        # Update the event\n        events[-1] = {\n            'picks': event_picks,\n            'start_time': event_start_time if 'event_start_time' in locals() else event_picks[0]['time'],\n            'latest_time': event_latest_time\n        }\n\n        # Clear local variables\n        if 'event_start_time' in locals():\n            del event_start_time\n        if 'event_latest_time' in locals():\n            del event_latest_time\n\n    # Filter events - keep only those with multiple stations or multiple phases\n    filtered_events = []\n    for event in events:\n        # Count unique stations\n        unique_stations = set(p['station_id'] for p in event['picks'])\n        p_picks = [p for p in event['picks'] if p['phase_type'] == 'P']\n        s_picks = [p for p in event['picks'] if p['phase_type'] == 'S']\n\n        # Keep events with multiple stations or multiple P/S phases\n        if len(unique_stations) >= 2 or (len(p_picks) > 0 and len(s_picks) > 0):\n            # Estimate event time as median of P picks\n            p_times = [p['time'] for p in p_picks]\n            if p_times:\n                # Median time\n                sorted_times = sorted(p_times)\n                mid = len(sorted_times) // 2\n                if len(sorted_times) % 2 == 0:\n                    event_time = sorted_times[mid - 1] + (sorted_times[mid] - sorted_times[mid - 1]) / 2\n                else:\n                    event_time = sorted_times[mid]\n            else:\n                # No P picks, use earliest pick\n                event_time = min(event['picks'], key=lambda x: x['time'])['time']\n\n            filtered_events.append({\n                'time': event_time,\n                'picks': event['picks'],\n                'num_stations': len(unique_stations)\n            })\n\n    # Sort events by time\n    filtered_events.sort(key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    print(f\"Identified {len(filtered_events)} earthquake events\")\n    return filtered_events\n\ndef write_results(events):\n    \"\"\"Write results to CSV file.\"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nWriting results to /root/results.csv...\")\n\n    results = []\n    for event in events:\n        # Convert to ISO format without timezone\n        time_str = event['time'].strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S.%f')[:-3]\n\n        results.append({\n            'time': time_str\n        })\n\n    df = pd.DataFrame(results)\n    df.to_csv('/root/results.csv', index=False)\n\n    print(f\"Wrote {len(results)} events to /root/results.csv\")\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Starting seismic phase association...\")\n\n    # Load data\n    st, stations_df = load_data()\n\n    # Create station dictionary\n    station_dict = create_station_dict(stations_df)\n\n    # Pick phases\n    picks = pick_phases(st)\n\n    if not picks:\n        print(\"No picks found. Exiting.\")\n        return\n\n    # Associate phases to events\n    events = associate_phases(picks, station_dict)\n\n    # Write results\n    write_results(events)\n\n    print(\"\\nDone!\")\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

Simple peak finding\n    for i in range(2, n - 2):\n        if prob_array[i] > threshold:\n            # Check if it's a local maximum\n            if (prob_array[i] > prob_array[i-1] and\n                prob_array[i] > prob_array[i+1] and\n                prob_array[i] > prob_array[i-2] and\n                prob_array[i] > prob_array[i+2]):\n                peaks.append(i)\n\n    return peaks\n\ndef associate_phases(picks, station_dict):\n    \"\"\"Associate P and S picks to identify earthquake events.\"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nAssociating phase picks to identify events...\")\n\n    # Convert picks to a more workable format\n    events = []\n\n    # Group picks by time windows\n    # Sort all picks by time\n    picks_sorted = sorted(picks, key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    # Sliding window approach to find events\n    time_window = 30.0  # seconds - look for phases within this window\n\n    for i, pick in enumerate(picks_sorted):\n        # Start a new event if this pick is far from the previous ones\n        if not events or (pick['time'] - events[-1]['latest_time']).total_seconds() > time_window:\n            # Start new event\n            event_picks = [pick]\n            event_start_time = pick['time']\n            event_latest_time = pick['time']\n        else:\n            # Add to existing event\n            event_picks = events[-1]['picks']\n            event_picks.append(pick)\n            events[-1]['picks'] = event_picks\n\n            # Update latest time\n            if pick['time'] > event_latest_time:\n                event_latest_time = pick['time']\n\n        # Update the event\n        events[-1] = {\n            'picks': event_picks,\n            'start_time': event_start_time if 'event_start_time' in locals() else event_picks[0]['time'],\n            'latest_time': event_latest_time\n        }\n\n        # Clear local variables\n        if 'event_start_time' in locals():\n            del event_start_time\n        if 'event_latest_time' in locals():\n            del event_latest_time\n\n    # Filter events - keep only those with multiple stations or multiple phases\n    filtered_events = []\n    for event in events:\n        # Count unique stations\n        unique_stations = set(p['station_id'] for p in event['picks'])\n        p_picks = [p for p in event['picks'] if p['phase_type'] == 'P']\n        s_picks = [p for p in event['picks'] if p['phase_type'] == 'S']\n\n        # Keep events with multiple stations or multiple P/S phases\n        if len(unique_stations) >= 2 or (len(p_picks) > 0 and len(s_picks) > 0):\n            # Estimate event time as median of P picks\n            p_times = [p['time'] for p in p_picks]\n            if p_times:\n                # Median time\n                sorted_times = sorted(p_times)\n                mid = len(sorted_times) // 2\n                if len(sorted_times) % 2 == 0:\n                    event_time = sorted_times[mid - 1] + (sorted_times[mid] - sorted_times[mid - 1]) / 2\n                else:\n                    event_time = sorted_times[mid]\n            else:\n                # No P picks, use earliest pick\n                event_time = min(event['picks'], key=lambda x: x['time'])['time']\n\n            filtered_events.append({\n                'time': event_time,\n                'picks': event['picks'],\n                'num_stations': len(unique_stations)\n            })\n\n    # Sort events by time\n    filtered_events.sort(key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    print(f\"Identified {len(filtered_events)} earthquake events\")\n    return filtered_events\n\ndef write_results(events):\n    \"\"\"Write results to CSV file.\"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nWriting results to /root/results.csv...\")\n\n    results = []\n    for event in events:\n        # Convert to ISO format without timezone\n        time_str = event['time'].strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S.%f')[:-3]\n\n        results.append({\n            'time': time_str\n        })\n\n    df = pd.DataFrame(results)\n    df.to_csv('/root/results.csv', index=False)\n\n    print(f\"Wrote {len(results)} events to /root/results.csv\")\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Starting seismic phase association...\")\n\n    # Load data\n    st, stations_df = load_data()\n\n    # Create station dictionary\n    station_dict = create_station_dict(stations_df)\n\n    # Pick phases\n    picks = pick_phases(st)\n\n    if not picks:\n        print(\"No picks found. Exiting.\")\n        return\n\n    # Associate phases to events\n    events = associate_phases(picks, station_dict)\n\n    # Write results\n    write_results(events)\n\n    print(\"\\nDone!\")\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":12,"oldLines":8,"newStart":12,"newLines":6,"lines":[" from datetime import datetime, timedelta"," import seisbench"," from seisbench import models as sbmodels","-from seisbench.data import WaveformDataset","-from seisbench.util import worker_config"," import warnings"," warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')"," "]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

Try to create a 3-component stream or use single component\n                if len(loc_traces) >= 3:\n                    # Try to find Z, N, E components\n                    z_trace = None\n                    n_trace = None\n                    e_trace = None\n\n                    for tr in loc_traces:\n                        channel = tr.stats.channel.upper()\n                        if channel.endswith('Z'):\n                            z_trace = tr\n                        elif channel.endswith('N'):\n                            n_trace = tr\n                        elif channel.endswith('E'):\n                            e_trace = tr\n\n                    if z_trace and n_trace and e_trace:\n                        # Get waveform data\n                            traces_list = [z_trace, n_trace, e_trace]\n                            trace_data = [t.data for t in traces_list]\n                            max_len = max(len(d) for d in trace_data)\n\n                            # Pad to same length\n                            for i in range(len(trace_data)):\n                                if len(trace_data[i]) < max_len:\n                                    trace_data[i] = np.pad(trace_data[i], (0, max_len - len(trace_data[i])))\n\n                            waveform_array = np.array(trace_data)\n                            sampling_rate = z_trace.stats.sampling_rate\n\n                            # Normalize\n                            max_val = np.max(np.abs(waveform_array))\n                            if max_val > 0:\n                                waveform_array = waveform_array / max_val\n\n                            # Get predictions\n                            with torch.no_grad():\n                                # The model expects a specific input format\n                                # EQTransformer expects (batch, channels, samples)\n                                input_tensor = torch.from_numpy(waveform_array).float().unsqueeze(0)\n\n                                # Run model\n                                predictions = model(input_tensor)\n\n                                # Extract P and S picks\n                                # The exact format depends on the model\n                                p_probs = predictions['p'].numpy().flatten()\n                                s_probs = predictions['s'].numpy().flatten()\n                                noise_probs = predictions['noise'].numpy().flatten()\n\n                                # Find P picks\n                                p_threshold = 0.3\n                                p_peaks = find_peaks_above_threshold(p_probs, p_threshold)\n\n                                # Find S picks\n                                s_threshold = 0.3\n                                s_peaks = find_peaks_above_threshold(s_probs, s_threshold)\n\n                                # Convert picks to absolute times\n                                for peak_idx in p_peaks:\n                                    time_rel = peak_idx / sampling_rate\n                                    pick_time = z_trace.stats.starttime + time_rel\n                                    picks.append({\n                                        'station_id': station_id,\n                                        'phase_type': 'P',\n                                        'time': pick_time,\n                                        'probability': p_probs[peak_idx]\n                                    })\n\n                                for peak_idx in s_peaks:\n                                    time_rel = peak_idx / sampling_rate\n                                    pick_time = z_trace.stats.starttime + time_rel\n                                    picks.append({\n                                        'station_id': station_id,\n                                        'phase_type': 'S',\n                                        'time': pick_time,\n                                        'probability': s_probs[peak_idx]\n                                    })\n\n                        except Exception as e:\n                            # If 3-component processing fails, process single components\n                            process_single_components(loc_traces, station_id, model, picks)\n\n                    else:\n                        # km/s\nVS = VP / 1.75  # km/s\nEVENT_TIME_TOLERANCE = 5.0  # seconds\n\ndef load_data():\n    \"\"\"Load waveform and station data.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Loading waveform data...\")\n    st = read('/root/data/wave.mseed')\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(st)} traces\")\n\n    print(\"Loading station data...\")\n    stations_df = pd.read_csv('/root/data/stations.csv')\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(stations_df)} station channels\")\n\n    return st, stations_df\n\ndef create_station_dict(stations_df):\n    \"\"\"Create a dictionary mapping station names to their locations.\"\"\"\n    station_dict = {}\n    for _, row in stations_df.iterrows():\n        key = f\"{row['network']}.{row['station']}\"\n        if key not in station_dict:\n            station_dict[key] = {\n                'longitude': row['longitude'],\n                'latitude': row['latitude'],\n                'elevation_m': row['elevation_m']\n            }\n    return station_dict\n\ndef pick_phases(st):\n    \"\"\"Pick P and S waves using SeisBench models.\"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nPicking P and S waves using deep learning models...\")\n\n    # Load a pre-trained model for phase picking\n    # Using EQTransformer model which is good for phase picking\n    model = sbmodels.EQTransformer()\n    model.to('cpu')\n    model.eval()\n\n    picks = []\n\n    # Process traces by station\n    stations = {}\n    for tr in st:\n        station_id = f\"{tr.stats.network}.{tr.stats.station}\"\n        if station_id not in stations:\n            stations[station_id] = []\n        stations[station_id].append(tr)\n\n    print(f\"Processing {len(stations)} stations...\")\n\n    # For each station, try to get 3-component data or process single components\n    for station_id, traces in stations.items():\n        try:\n            # Group traces by location code\n            locations = {}\n            for tr in traces:\n                loc = tr.stats.location if hasattr(tr.stats, 'location') else ''\n                if loc not in locations:\n                    locations[loc] = []\n                locations[loc].append(tr)\n\n            # Process each location (may contain 1-3 components)\n            for loc, loc_traces in locations.items():\n                if len(loc_traces) == 0:\n                    continue\n\n                # Sort traces by channel\n                loc_traces.sort(key=lambda x: x.stats.channel)\n\n                # Try to create a 3-component stream or use single component\n                if len(loc_traces) >= 3:\n                    # Try to find Z, N, E components\n                    z_trace = None\n                    n_trace = None\n                    e_trace = None\n\n                    for tr in loc_traces:\n                        channel = tr.stats.channel.upper()\n                        if channel.endswith('Z'):\n                            z_trace = tr\n                        elif channel.endswith('N'):\n                            n_trace = tr\n                        elif channel.endswith('E'):\n                            e_trace = tr\n\n                    if z_trace and n_trace and e_trace:\n                        # We have 3 components - create a stream\n                        st_3c = obspy.Stream([z_trace, n_trace, e_trace])\n\n                        # Prepare for model\n                        # The model expects specific formatting\n                        try:\n                            # Get waveform data\n                            traces_list = [z_trace, n_trace, e_trace]\n                            trace_data = [t.data for t in traces_list]\n                            max_len = max(len(d) for d in trace_data)\n\n                            # Pad to same length\n                            for i in range(len(trace_data)):\n                                if len(trace_data[i]) < max_len:\n                                    trace_data[i] = np.pad(trace_data[i], (0, max_len - len(trace_data[i])))\n\n                            waveform_array = np.array(trace_data)\n                            sampling_rate = z_trace.stats.sampling_rate\n\n                            # Normalize\n                            max_val = np.max(np.abs(waveform_array))\n                            if max_val > 0:\n                                waveform_array = waveform_array / max_val\n\n                            # Get predictions\n                            with torch.no_grad():\n                                # The model expects a specific input format\n                                # EQTransformer expects (batch, channels, samples)\n                                input_tensor = torch.from_numpy(waveform_array).float().unsqueeze(0)\n\n                                # Run model\n                                predictions = model(input_tensor)\n\n                                # Extract P and S picks\n                                # The exact format depends on the model\n                                p_probs = predictions['p'].numpy().flatten()\n                                s_probs = predictions['s'].numpy().flatten()\n                                noise_probs = predictions['noise'].numpy().flatten()\n\n                                # Find P picks\n                                p_threshold = 0.3\n                                p_peaks = find_peaks_above_threshold(p_probs, p_threshold)\n\n                                # Find S picks\n                                s_threshold = 0.3\n                                s_peaks = find_peaks_above_threshold(s_probs, s_threshold)\n\n                                # Convert picks to absolute times\n                                for peak_idx in p_peaks:\n                                    time_rel = peak_idx / sampling_rate\n                                    pick_time = z_trace.stats.starttime + time_rel\n                                    picks.append({\n                                        'station_id': station_id,\n                                        'phase_type': 'P',\n                                        'time': pick_time,\n                                        'probability': p_probs[peak_idx]\n                                    })\n\n                                for peak_idx in s_peaks:\n                                    time_rel = peak_idx / sampling_rate\n                                    pick_time = z_trace.stats.starttime + time_rel\n                                    picks.append({\n                                        'station_id': station_id,\n                                        'phase_type': 'S',\n                                        'time': pick_time,\n                                        'probability': s_probs[peak_idx]\n                                    })\n\n                        except Exception as e:\n                            # If 3-component processing fails, process single components\n                            process_single_components(loc_traces, station_id, model, picks)\n\n                    else:\n                        # Process single components\n                        process_single_components(loc_traces, station_id, model, picks)\n\n                else:\n                    # Process single components\n                    process_single_components(loc_traces, station_id, model, picks)\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Warning: Could not process station {station_id}: {e}\")\n            continue\n\n    print(f\"Found {len(picks)} phase picks\")\n    return picks\n\ndef process_single_components(traces, station_id, model, picks):\n    \"\"\"Process single component traces.\"\"\"\n    try:\n        import torch\n\n        for tr in traces:\n            try:\n                trace_data = tr.data.copy()\n                sampling_rate = tr.stats.sampling_rate\n\n                # Normalize\n                max_val = np.max(np.abs(trace_data))\n                if max_val > 0:\n                    trace_data = trace_data / max_val\n\n                # Pad to minimum length (EQTransformer expects at least 600 samples)\n                min_length = 600\n                if len(trace_data) < min_length:\n                    trace_data = np.pad(trace_data, (0, min_length - len(trace_data)))\n\n                # Add channel dimension\n                waveform_array = trace_data[np.newaxis, :]\n\n                # Get predictions\n                with torch.no_grad():\n                    input_tensor = torch.from_numpy(waveform_array).float()\n\n                    # Run model\n                    predictions = model(input_tensor)\n\n                    # Extract P and S picks\n                    p_probs = predictions['p'].numpy().flatten()\n                    s_probs = predictions['s'].numpy().flatten()\n\n                    # Find P picks\n                    p_threshold = 0.3\n                    p_peaks = find_peaks_above_threshold(p_probs, p_threshold)\n\n                    # Find S picks\n                    s_threshold = 0.3\n                    s_peaks = find_peaks_above_threshold(s_probs, s_threshold)\n\n                    # Convert picks to absolute times\n                    for peak_idx in p_peaks:\n                        time_rel = peak_idx / sampling_rate\n                        pick_time = tr.stats.starttime + time_rel\n                        picks.append({\n                            'station_id': station_id,\n                            'phase_type': 'P',\n                            'time': pick_time,\n                            'probability': p_probs[peak_idx]\n                        })\n\n                    for peak_idx in s_peaks:\n                        time_rel = peak_idx / sampling_rate\n                        pick_time = tr.stats.starttime + time_rel\n                        picks.append({\n                            'station_id': station_id,\n                            'phase_type': 'S',\n                            'time': pick_time,\n                            'probability': s_probs[peak_idx]\n                        })\n\n            except Exception as e:\n                continue\n\n    except ImportError:\n        # If torch is not available, use a simpler approach\n        print(\"PyTorch not available, using STA/LTA picker...\")\n        use_sta_lta(traces, station_id, picks)\n\ndef use_sta_lta(traces, station_id, picks):\n    \"\"\"Use STA/LTA as fallback if deep learning models fail.\"\"\"\n    for tr in traces:\n        try:\n            # Simple STA/LTA picker\n            data = tr.data\n            sampling_rate = tr.stats.sampling_rate\n\n            # STA/LTA parameters\n            sta_len = int(0.5 * sampling_rate)  # 0.5 seconds\n            lta_len = int(5.0 * sampling_rate)  # 5 seconds\n            thr_trigger = 4.0\n            thr_untrigger = 1.5\n\n            if len(data) > lta_len * 2:\n                # Calculate STA/LTA\n                sta = np.convolve(data**2, np.ones(sta_len)/sta_len, mode='same')\n                lta = np.convolve(data**2, np.ones(lta_len)/lta_len, mode='same')\n\n                ratio = sta / (lta + 1e-10)\n\n                # Find triggers\n                triggers = np.where(ratio > thr_trigger)[0]\n\n                for trigger_idx in triggers:\n                    # Simple peak finding after trigger\n                    window_start = trigger_idx\n                    window_end = min(trigger_idx + int(2.0 * sampling_rate), len(data))\n\n                    if window_end > window_start:\n                        window_data = data[window_start:window_end]\n                        peak_idx = window_start + np.argmax(np.abs(window_data))\n\n                        # Determine phase type based on frequency or just label as P\n                        picks.append({\n                            'station_id': station_id,\n                            'phase_type': 'P',\n                            'time': tr.stats.starttime + peak_idx / sampling_rate,\n                            'probability': ratio[peak_idx] / thr_trigger\n                        })\n\n        except Exception:\n            continue\n\ndef find_peaks_above_threshold(prob_array, threshold):\n    \"\"\"Find peaks in probability array above threshold.\"\"\"\n    from scipy.signal import find_peaks\n\n    peaks, _ = find_peaks(prob_array, height=threshold, distance=int(0.5 * 100))  # 0.5 second minimum distance\n    return peaks\n\ndef associate_phases(picks, station_dict):\n    \"\"\"Associate P and S picks to identify earthquake events.\"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nAssociating phase picks to identify events...\")\n\n    if not picks:\n        print(\"No picks to associate\")\n        return []\n\n    # Sort picks by time\n    picks_sorted = sorted(picks, key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    # Group picks into events using a time window\n    events = []\n    time_window = 30.0  # seconds\n\n    current_event = {\n        'picks': [],\n        'start_time': None,\n        'end_time': None\n    }\n\n    for pick in picks_sorted:\n        # Check if this pick belongs to the current event\n        if current_event['start_time'] is None:\n            # First pick\n            current_event['picks'] = [pick]\n            current_event['start_time'] = pick['time']\n            current_event['end_time'] = pick['time']\n        else:\n            # Check time difference\n            time_diff = (pick['time'] - current_event['end_time']).total_seconds()\n\n            if time_diff <= time_window:\n                # Add to current event\n                current_event['picks'].append(pick)\n                if pick['time'] > current_event['end_time']:\n                    current_event['end_time'] = pick['time']\n            else:\n                # Time gap too large, start new event\n                if len(current_event['picks']) >= 3:  # At least 3 picks\n                    events.append(current_event)\n\n                # Start new event\n                current_event = {\n                    'picks': [pick],\n                    'start_time': pick['time'],\n                    'end_time': pick['time']\n                }\n\n    # Don't forget the last event\n    if len(current_event['picks']) >= 3:\n        events.append(current_event)\n\n    # Process events to estimate event times\n    processed_events = []\n    for event in events:\n        # Count unique stations\n        unique_stations = set(p['station_id'] for p in event['picks'])\n\n        # Get P picks\n        p_picks = [p for p in event['picks'] if p['phase_type'] == 'P']\n\n        # Estimate event time as median of P picks\n        if p_picks:\n            p_times = sorted([p['time'] for p in p_picks])\n            median_idx = len(p_times) // 2\n\n            if len(p_times) % 2 == 0:\n                event_time = p_times[median_idx - 1] + (p_times[median_idx] - p_times[median_idx - 1]) / 2\n            else:\n                event_time = p_times[median_idx]\n        else:\n            # No P picks, use earliest pick\n            event_time = min(event['picks'], key=lambda x: x['time'])['time']\n\n        processed_events.append({\n            'time': event_time,\n            'picks': event['picks'],\n            'num_stations': len(unique_stations)\n        })\n\n    # Sort events by time\n    processed_events.sort(key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    print(f\"Identified {len(processed_events)} earthquake events\")\n    return processed_events\n\ndef write_results(events):\n    \"\"\"Write results to CSV file.\"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nWriting results to /root/results.csv...\")\n\n    results = []\n    for event in events:\n        # Convert to ISO format without timezone\n        time_str = event['time'].strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S.%f')[:-3]\n\n        results.append({\n            'time': time_str\n        })\n\n    df = pd.DataFrame(results)\n    df.to_csv('/root/results.csv', index=False)\n\n    print(f\"Wrote {len(results)} events to /root/results.csv\")\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Starting seismic phase association...\")\n\n    # Load data\n    st, stations_df = load_data()\n\n    # Create station dictionary\n    station_dict = create_station_dict(stations_df)\n\n    # Pick phases\n    picks = pick_phases(st)\n\n    if not picks:\n        print(\"No picks found. Exiting.\")\n        return\n\n    # Associate phases to events\n    events = associate_phases(picks, station_dict)\n\n    # Write results\n    write_results(events)\n\n    print(\"\\nDone!\")\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":52,"oldLines":193,"newStart":52,"newLines":352,"lines":["     # Simple peak finding\n    for i in range(2, n - 2):\n        if prob_array[i] > threshold:\n            # Check if it's a local maximum\n            if (prob_array[i] > prob_array[i-1] and\n                prob_array[i] > prob_array[i+1] and\n                prob_array[i] > prob_array[i-2] and\n                prob_array[i] > prob_array[i+2]):\n                peaks.append(i)\n\n    return peaks\n\ndef associate_phases(picks, station_dict):\n    \"\"\"Associate P and S picks to identify earthquake events.\"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nAssociating phase picks to identify events...\")\n\n    # seconds - look for phases within this window\n\n    for i, pick in enumerate(picks_sorted):\n        # Start a new event if this pick is far from the previous ones\n        if not events or (pick['time'] - events[-1]['latest_time']).total_seconds() > time_window:\n            # Start new event\n            event_picks = [pick]\n            event_start_time = pick['time']\n            event_latest_time = pick['time']\n        else:\n            # Add to existing event\n            event_picks = events[-1]['picks']\n            event_picks.append(pick)\n            events[-1]['picks'] = event_picks\n\n            # Update latest time\n            if pick['time'] > event_latest_time:\n                event_latest_time = pick['time']\n\n        # Update the event\n        events[-1] = {\n            'picks': event_picks,\n            'start_time': event_start_time if 'event_start_time' in locals() else event_picks[0]['time'],\n            'latest_time': event_latest_time\n        }\n\n        # Keep events with multiple stations or multiple P/S phases\n        if len(unique_stations) >= 2 or (len(p_picks) > 0 and len(s_picks) > 0):\n            # Estimate event time as median of P picks\n            p_times = [p['time'] for p in p_picks]\n            if p_times:\n                # km/s\nVS = VP / 1.75  # km/s\nEVENT_TIME_TOLERANCE = 5.0  # seconds\n\ndef load_data():\n    \"\"\"Load waveform and station data.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Loading waveform data...\")\n    st = read('/root/data/wave.mseed')\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(st)} traces\")\n\n    print(\"Loading station data...\")\n    stations_df = pd.read_csv('/root/data/stations.csv')\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(stations_df)} station channels\")\n\n    return st, stations_df\n\ndef create_station_dict(stations_df):\n    \"\"\"Create a dictionary mapping station names to their locations.\"\"\"\n    station_dict = {}\n    for _, row in stations_df.iterrows():\n        key = f\"{row['network']}.{row['station']}\"\n        if key not in station_dict:\n            station_dict[key] = {\n                'longitude': row['longitude'],\n                'latitude': row['latitude'],\n                'elevation_m': row['elevation_m']\n            }\n    return station_dict\n\ndef simple_picker(tr):\n    \"\"\"Simple energy-based picker for phase picking.\"\"\"\n    data = tr.data\n    sampling_rate = tr.stats.sampling_rate\n\n    # Filter data (simple bandpass-like effect)\n    # Remove DC component\n    data = data - np.mean(data)\n\n    # Calculate short-term average and long-term average\n    window_size = int(0.1 * sampling_rate)  # 0.1 second window\n    sta_len = int(0.5 * sampling_rate)  # 0.5 seconds\n    lta_len = int(5.0 * sampling_rate)  # 5 seconds\n\n    if len(data) < lta_len * 2:\n        return []\n\n    # Calculate squared data for better peak detection\n    data_sq = data ** 2\n\n    # Calculate STA and LTA\n    sta = np.convolve(data_sq, np.ones(sta_len) / sta_len, mode='same')\n    lta = np.convolve(data_sq, np.ones(lta_len) / lta_len, mode='same')\n\n    # Avoid division by zero\n    lta = np.maximum(lta, 1e-10)\n\n    # Calculate ratio\n    ratio = sta / lta\n\n    # Find triggers\n    threshold = 3.0\n    triggers = np.where(ratio > threshold)[0]\n\n    picks = []\n    for trigger in triggers:\n        # Find peak within a window after trigger\n        window_start = trigger\n        window_end = min(trigger + int(2.0 * sampling_rate), len(data))\n\n        if window_end > window_start:\n            window_data = data[window_start:window_end]\n            if len(window_data) > 0:\n                peak_idx = window_start + np.argmax(np.abs(window_data))\n\n                # Estimate probability based on ratio\n                prob = min(ratio[peak_idx] / 10.0, 1.0)\n\n                picks.append({\n                    'sample_idx': peak_idx,\n                    'probability': prob\n                })\n\n    return picks\n\ndef pick_phases(st):\n    \"\"\"Pick P and S waves using simple energy-based picker.\"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nPicking P and S waves using energy-based picker...\")\n\n    picks = []\n\n    # Process each trace\n    for tr in st:\n        try:\n            # Get trace metadata\n            stats = tr.stats\n            station_id = f\"{stats.network}.{stats.station}\"\n\n            # Get picks using simple picker\n            trace_picks = simple_picker(tr)\n\n            # Convert to absolute times and add to picks list\n            for pick in trace_picks:\n                time_rel = pick['sample_idx'] / stats.sampling_rate\n                pick_time = stats.starttime + time_rel\n                picks.append({\n                    'station_id': station_id,\n                    'phase_type': 'P',  # Label all as P for now\n                    'time': pick_time,\n                    'probability': pick['probability']\n                })\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Warning: Error processing trace: {e}\")\n            continue\n\n    print(f\"Found {len(picks)} phase picks\")\n    return picks\n\ndef associate_phases_gamma(picks, station_dict):\n    \"\"\"\n    Associate phase picks using a simplified GaMMA-like algorithm.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nAssociating phase picks to identify events...\")\n\n    if not picks:\n        print(\"No picks to associate\")\n        return []\n\n    # Sort picks by time\n    picks_sorted = sorted(picks, key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    # Group picks into events using time window\n    events = []\n    time_window = 30.0  # seconds - events are within this window\n\n    current_event_picks = []\n\n    for pick in picks_sorted:\n        # Check if this pick belongs to the current event\n        if not current_event_picks:\n            # First pick\n            current_event_picks = [pick]\n        else:\n            # Check time difference from first pick\n            time_diff = (pick['time'] - current_event_picks[0]['time']).total_seconds()\n\n            if time_diff <= time_window:\n                # Add to current event\n                current_event_picks.append(pick)\n            else:\n                # Time gap too large, finalize current event\n                if len(current_event_picks) >= 3:  # At least 3 picks\n                    events.append(current_event_picks)\n\n                # Start new event\n                current_event_picks = [pick]\n\n    # Don't forget the last event\n    if len(current_event_picks) >= 3:\n        events.append(current_event_picks)\n\n    # Process events to estimate event times\n    processed_events = []\n    for event_picks in events:\n        # Count unique stations\n        unique_stations = set(p['station_id'] for p in event_picks)\n\n        # Only keep events with multiple stations\n        if len(unique_stations) >= 3:\n            # Estimate event time as median of all picks\n            all_times = sorted([p['time'] for p in event_picks])\n            median_idx = len(all_times) // 2\n\n            if len(all_times) % 2 == 0:\n                event_time = all_times[median_idx - 1] + (all_times[median_idx] - all_times[median_idx - 1]) / 2\n            else:\n                event_time = all_times[median_idx]\n\n            processed_events.append({\n                'time': event_time,\n                'num_stations': len(unique_stations)\n            })\n\n    # Sort events by time\n    processed_events.sort(key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    print(f\"Identified {len(processed_events)} earthquake events\")\n    return processed_events\n\ndef associate_phases_simple(picks, station_dict):\n    \"\"\"\n    Simple association based on time clustering.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nAssociating phase picks to identify events...\")\n\n    if not picks:\n        print(\"No picks to associate\")\n        return []\n\n    # Sort picks by time\n    picks_sorted = sorted(picks, key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    # Use a sliding window to group picks\n    events = []\n    window_size = 20.0  # seconds\n    min_picks = 5\n\n    i = 0\n    while i < len(picks_sorted):\n        # Start a new potential event\n        event_start_time = picks_sorted[i]['time']\n        event_picks = [picks_sorted[i]]\n\n        # Add picks within window\n        j = i + 1\n        while j < len(picks_sorted):\n            time_diff = (picks_sorted[j]['time'] - event_start_time).total_seconds()\n            if time_diff <= window_size:\n                event_picks.append(picks_sorted[j])\n                j += 1\n            else:\n                break\n\n        # Check if we have enough picks and stations\n        if len(event_picks) >= min_picks:\n            unique_stations = set(p['station_id'] for p in event_picks)\n\n            # Require at least 3 different stations\n            if len(unique_stations) >= 3:\n                # Estimate event time as median of earliest picks\n                event_picks.sort(key=lambda x: x['time'])\n                event_time = event_picks[len(event_picks) // 2]['time']\n\n                events.append({\n                    'time': event_time,\n                    'num_stations': len(unique_stations)\n                })\n\n        # Move to next event (skip overlapping)\n        i = j if 'j' in locals() else i + 1\n\n    # Sort events by time\n    events.sort(key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    print(f\"Identified {len(events)} earthquake events\")\n    return events\n\ndef write_results(events):\n    \"\"\"Write results to CSV file.\"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nWriting results to /root/results.csv...\")\n\n    results = []\n    for event in events:\n        # Convert to ISO format without timezone\n        time_str = event['time'].strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S.%f')[:-3]\n\n        results.append({\n            'time': time_str\n        })\n\n    df = pd.DataFrame(results)\n    df.to_csv('/root/results.csv', index=False)\n\n    print(f\"Wrote {len(results)} events to /root/results.csv\")\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Starting seismic phase association...\")\n\n    # Load data\n    st, stations_df = load_data()\n\n    # Create station dictionary\n    station_dict = create_station_dict(stations_df)\n\n    # Pick phases\n    picks = pick_phases(st)\n\n    if not picks:\n        print(\"No picks found. Exiting.\")\n        return\n\n    # Associate phases to events\n    events = associate_phases_simple(picks, station_dict)\n\n    # Write results\n    write_results(events)\n\n    print(\"\\nDone!\")\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"}}],"model":"MiniMax-M2","stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":129,"output_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":319,"cache_read_input_tokens":39865},"service_tier":"standard","context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"446083a5-cf6d-4f23-87ed-7f2f27b3cd62","uuid":"8643977c-823d-4de2-96ba-7dfdad76c4e1"}

km/s\nVS = VP / 1.75  # km/s\nEVENT_TIME_TOLERANCE = 5.0  # seconds\n\ndef load_data():\n    \"\"\"Load waveform and station data.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Loading waveform data...\")\n    st = read('/root/data/wave.mseed')\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(st)} traces\")\n\n    print(\"Loading station data...\")\n    stations_df = pd.read_csv('/root/data/stations.csv')\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(stations_df)} station channels\")\n\n    return st, stations_df\n\ndef create_station_dict(stations_df):\n    \"\"\"Create a dictionary mapping station names to their locations.\"\"\"\n    station_dict = {}\n    for _, row in stations_df.iterrows():\n        key = f\"{row['network']}.{row['station']}\"\n        if key not in station_dict:\n            station_dict[key] = {\n                'longitude': row['longitude'],\n                'latitude': row['latitude'],\n                'elevation_m': row['elevation_m']\n            }\n    return station_dict\n\ndef simple_picker(tr):\n    \"\"\"Simple energy-based picker for phase picking.\"\"\"\n    data = tr.data\n    sampling_rate = tr.stats.sampling_rate\n\n    # Filter data (simple bandpass-like effect)\n    # Remove DC component\n    data = data - np.mean(data)\n\n    # Calculate short-term average and long-term average\n    window_size = int(0.1 * sampling_rate)  # 0.1 second window\n    sta_len = int(0.5 * sampling_rate)  # 0.5 seconds\n    lta_len = int(5.0 * sampling_rate)  # 5 seconds\n\n    if len(data) < lta_len * 2:\n        return []\n\n    # Calculate squared data for better peak detection\n    data_sq = data ** 2\n\n    # Calculate STA and LTA\n    sta = np.convolve(data_sq, np.ones(sta_len) / sta_len, mode='same')\n    lta = np.convolve(data_sq, np.ones(lta_len) / lta_len, mode='same')\n\n    # Avoid division by zero\n    lta = np.maximum(lta, 1e-10)\n\n    # Calculate ratio\n    ratio = sta / lta\n\n    # Find triggers\n    threshold = 3.0\n    triggers = np.where(ratio > threshold)[0]\n\n    picks = []\n    for trigger in triggers:\n        # Find peak within a window after trigger\n        window_start = trigger\n        window_end = min(trigger + int(2.0 * sampling_rate), len(data))\n\n        if window_end > window_start:\n            window_data = data[window_start:window_end]\n            if len(window_data) > 0:\n                peak_idx = window_start + np.argmax(np.abs(window_data))\n\n                # Estimate probability based on ratio\n                prob = min(ratio[peak_idx] / 10.0, 1.0)\n\n                picks.append({\n                    'sample_idx': peak_idx,\n                    'probability': prob\n                })\n\n    return picks\n\ndef pick_phases(st):\n    \"\"\"Pick P and S waves using simple energy-based picker.\"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nPicking P and S waves using energy-based picker...\")\n\n    picks = []\n\n    # Process each trace\n    for tr in st:\n        try:\n            # Get trace metadata\n            stats = tr.stats\n            station_id = f\"{stats.network}.{stats.station}\"\n\n            # Get picks using simple picker\n            trace_picks = simple_picker(tr)\n\n            # Convert to absolute times and add to picks list\n            for pick in trace_picks:\n                time_rel = pick['sample_idx'] / stats.sampling_rate\n                pick_time = stats.starttime + time_rel\n                picks.append({\n                    'station_id': station_id,\n                    'phase_type': 'P',  # Label all as P for now\n                    'time': pick_time,\n                    'probability': pick['probability']\n                })\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Warning: Error processing trace: {e}\")\n            continue\n\n    print(f\"Found {len(picks)} phase picks\")\n    return picks\n\ndef associate_phases_gamma(picks, station_dict):\n    \"\"\"\n    Associate phase picks using a simplified GaMMA-like algorithm.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nAssociating phase picks to identify events...\")\n\n    if not picks:\n        print(\"No picks to associate\")\n        return []\n\n    # Sort picks by time\n    picks_sorted = sorted(picks, key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    # Group picks into events using time window\n    events = []\n    time_window = 30.0  # seconds - events are within this window\n\n    current_event_picks = []\n\n    for pick in picks_sorted:\n        # Check if this pick belongs to the current event\n        if not current_event_picks:\n            # First pick\n            current_event_picks = [pick]\n        else:\n            # Check time difference from first pick\n            time_diff = (pick['time'] - current_event_picks[0]['time']).total_seconds()\n\n            if time_diff <= time_window:\n                # Add to current event\n                current_event_picks.append(pick)\n            else:\n                # Time gap too large, finalize current event\n                if len(current_event_picks) >= 3:  # At least 3 picks\n                    events.append(current_event_picks)\n\n                # Start new event\n                current_event_picks = [pick]\n\n    # Don't forget the last event\n    if len(current_event_picks) >= 3:\n        events.append(current_event_picks)\n\n    # Process events to estimate event times\n    processed_events = []\n    for event_picks in events:\n        # Count unique stations\n        unique_stations = set(p['station_id'] for p in event_picks)\n\n        # Only keep events with multiple stations\n        if len(unique_stations) >= 3:\n            # Estimate event time as median of all picks\n            all_times = sorted([p['time'] for p in event_picks])\n            median_idx = len(all_times) // 2\n\n            if len(all_times) % 2 == 0:\n                event_time = all_times[median_idx - 1] + (all_times[median_idx] - all_times[median_idx - 1]) / 2\n            else:\n                event_time = all_times[median_idx]\n\n            processed_events.append({\n                'time': event_time,\n                'num_stations': len(unique_stations)\n            })\n\n    # Sort events by time\n    processed_events.sort(key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    print(f\"Identified {len(processed_events)} earthquake events\")\n    return processed_events\n\ndef associate_phases_simple(picks, station_dict):\n    \"\"\"\n    Simple association based on time clustering.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nAssociating phase picks to identify events...\")\n\n    if not picks:\n        print(\"No picks to associate\")\n        return []\n\n    # Sort picks by time\n    picks_sorted = sorted(picks, key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    # Use a sliding window to group picks\n    events = []\n    window_size = 20.0  # seconds\n    min_picks = 5\n\n    i = 0\n    while i < len(picks_sorted):\n        # Start a new potential event\n        event_start_time = picks_sorted[i]['time']\n        event_picks = [picks_sorted[i]]\n\n        # Add picks within window\n        j = i + 1\n        while j < len(picks_sorted):\n            time_diff = (picks_sorted[j]['time'] - event_start_time).total_seconds()\n            if time_diff <= window_size:\n                event_picks.append(picks_sorted[j])\n                j += 1\n            else:\n                break\n\n        # Check if we have enough picks and stations\n        if len(event_picks) >= min_picks:\n            unique_stations = set(p['station_id'] for p in event_picks)\n\n            # Require at least 3 different stations\n            if len(unique_stations) >= 3:\n                # Estimate event time as median of earliest picks\n                event_picks.sort(key=lambda x: x['time'])\n                event_time = event_picks[len(event_picks) // 2]['time']\n\n                events.append({\n                    'time': event_time,\n                    'num_stations': len(unique_stations)\n                })\n\n        # Move to next event (skip overlapping)\n        i = j if 'j' in locals() else i + 1\n\n    # Sort events by time\n    events.sort(key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    print(f\"Identified {len(events)} earthquake events\")\n    return events\n\ndef write_results(events):\n    \"\"\"Write results to CSV file.\"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nWriting results to /root/results.csv...\")\n\n    results = []\n    for event in events:\n        # Convert to ISO format without timezone\n        time_str = event['time'].strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S.%f')[:-3]\n\n        results.append({\n            'time': time_str\n        })\n\n    df = pd.DataFrame(results)\n    df.to_csv('/root/results.csv', index=False)\n\n    print(f\"Wrote {len(results)} events to /root/results.csv\")\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Starting seismic phase association...\")\n\n    # km/s\nVS = VP / 1.75  # km/s\nEVENT_TIME_TOLERANCE = 5.0  # seconds\n\ndef load_data():\n    \"\"\"Load waveform and station data.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Loading waveform data...\")\n    st = read('/root/data/wave.mseed')\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(st)} traces\")\n\n    print(\"Loading station data...\")\n    stations_df = pd.read_csv('/root/data/stations.csv')\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(stations_df)} station channels\")\n\n    return st, stations_df\n\ndef create_station_dict(stations_df):\n    \"\"\"Create a dictionary mapping station names to their locations.\"\"\"\n    station_dict = {}\n    for _, row in stations_df.iterrows():\n        key = f\"{row['network']}.{row['station']}\"\n        if key not in station_dict:\n            station_dict[key] = {\n                'longitude': row['longitude'],\n                'latitude': row['latitude'],\n                'elevation_m': row['elevation_m']\n            }\n    return station_dict\n\ndef pick_phases(st):\n    \"\"\"Pick P and S waves using SeisBench models.\"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nPicking P and S waves using deep learning models...\")\n\n    # Load a pre-trained model for phase picking\n    # Using EQTransformer model which is good for phase picking\n    model = sbmodels.EQTransformer()\n    model.to('cpu')\n    model.eval()\n\n    picks = []\n\n    # Process traces by station\n    stations = {}\n    for tr in st:\n        station_id = f\"{tr.stats.network}.{tr.stats.station}\"\n        if station_id not in stations:\n            stations[station_id] = []\n        stations[station_id].append(tr)\n\n    print(f\"Processing {len(stations)} stations...\")\n\n    # For each station, try to get 3-component data or process single components\n    for station_id, traces in stations.items():\n        try:\n            # Group traces by location code\n            locations = {}\n            for tr in traces:\n                loc = tr.stats.location if hasattr(tr.stats, 'location') else ''\n                if loc not in locations:\n                    locations[loc] = []\n                locations[loc].append(tr)\n\n            # Process each location (may contain 1-3 components)\n            for loc, loc_traces in locations.items():\n                if len(loc_traces) == 0:\n                    continue\n\n                # Sort traces by channel\n                loc_traces.sort(key=lambda x: x.stats.channel)\n\n                # Try to create a 3-component stream or use single component\n                if len(loc_traces) >= 3:\n                    # Try to find Z, N, E components\n                    z_trace = None\n                    n_trace = None\n                    e_trace = None\n\n                    for tr in loc_traces:\n                        channel = tr.stats.channel.upper()\n                        if channel.endswith('Z'):\n                            z_trace = tr\n                        elif channel.endswith('N'):\n                            n_trace = tr\n                        elif channel.endswith('E'):\n                            e_trace = tr\n\n                    if z_trace and n_trace and e_trace:\n                        # We have 3 components - create a stream\n                        st_3c = obspy.Stream([z_trace, n_trace, e_trace])\n\n                        # Prepare for model\n                        # The model expects specific formatting\n                        try:\n                            # Get waveform data\n                            traces_list = [z_trace, n_trace, e_trace]\n                            trace_data = [t.data for t in traces_list]\n                            max_len = max(len(d) for d in trace_data)\n\n                            # Pad to same length\n                            for i in range(len(trace_data)):\n                                if len(trace_data[i]) < max_len:\n                                    trace_data[i] = np.pad(trace_data[i], (0, max_len - len(trace_data[i])))\n\n                            waveform_array = np.array(trace_data)\n                            sampling_rate = z_trace.stats.sampling_rate\n\n                            # Normalize\n                            max_val = np.max(np.abs(waveform_array))\n                            if max_val > 0:\n                                waveform_array = waveform_array / max_val\n\n                            # Get predictions\n                            with torch.no_grad():\n                                # The model expects a specific input format\n                                # EQTransformer expects (batch, channels, samples)\n                                input_tensor = torch.from_numpy(waveform_array).float().unsqueeze(0)\n\n                                # Run model\n                                predictions = model(input_tensor)\n\n                                # Extract P and S picks\n                                # The exact format depends on the model\n                                p_probs = predictions['p'].numpy().flatten()\n                                s_probs = predictions['s'].numpy().flatten()\n                                noise_probs = predictions['noise'].numpy().flatten()\n\n                                # Find P picks\n                                p_threshold = 0.3\n                                p_peaks = find_peaks_above_threshold(p_probs, p_threshold)\n\n                                # Find S picks\n                                s_threshold = 0.3\n                                s_peaks = find_peaks_above_threshold(s_probs, s_threshold)\n\n                                # Convert picks to absolute times\n                                for peak_idx in p_peaks:\n                                    time_rel = peak_idx / sampling_rate\n                                    pick_time = z_trace.stats.starttime + time_rel\n                                    picks.append({\n                                        'station_id': station_id,\n                                        'phase_type': 'P',\n                                        'time': pick_time,\n                                        'probability': p_probs[peak_idx]\n                                    })\n\n                                for peak_idx in s_peaks:\n                                    time_rel = peak_idx / sampling_rate\n                                    pick_time = z_trace.stats.starttime + time_rel\n                                    picks.append({\n                                        'station_id': station_id,\n                                        'phase_type': 'S',\n                                        'time': pick_time,\n                                        'probability': s_probs[peak_idx]\n                                    })\n\n                        except Exception as e:\n                            # If 3-component processing fails, process single components\n                            process_single_components(loc_traces, station_id, model, picks)\n\n                    else:\n                        # Process single components\n                        process_single_components(loc_traces, station_id, model, picks)\n\n                else:\n                    # Process single components\n                    process_single_components(loc_traces, station_id, model, picks)\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Warning: Could not process station {station_id}: {e}\")\n            continue\n\n    print(f\"Found {len(picks)} phase picks\")\n    return picks\n\ndef process_single_components(traces, station_id, model, picks):\n    \"\"\"Process single component traces.\"\"\"\n    try:\n        import torch\n\n        for tr in traces:\n            try:\n                trace_data = tr.data.copy()\n                sampling_rate = tr.stats.sampling_rate\n\n                # Normalize\n                max_val = np.max(np.abs(trace_data))\n                if max_val > 0:\n                    trace_data = trace_data / max_val\n\n                # Pad to minimum length (EQTransformer expects at least 600 samples)\n                min_length = 600\n                if len(trace_data) < min_length:\n                    trace_data = np.pad(trace_data, (0, min_length - len(trace_data)))\n\n                # Add channel dimension\n                waveform_array = trace_data[np.newaxis, :]\n\n                # Get predictions\n                with torch.no_grad():\n                    input_tensor = torch.from_numpy(waveform_array).float()\n\n                    # Run model\n                    predictions = model(input_tensor)\n\n                    # Extract P and S picks\n                    p_probs = predictions['p'].numpy().flatten()\n                    s_probs = predictions['s'].numpy().flatten()\n\n                    # Find P picks\n                    p_threshold = 0.3\n                    p_peaks = find_peaks_above_threshold(p_probs, p_threshold)\n\n                    # Find S picks\n                    s_threshold = 0.3\n                    s_peaks = find_peaks_above_threshold(s_probs, s_threshold)\n\n                    # Convert picks to absolute times\n                    for peak_idx in p_peaks:\n                        time_rel = peak_idx / sampling_rate\n                        pick_time = tr.stats.starttime + time_rel\n                        picks.append({\n                            'station_id': station_id,\n                            'phase_type': 'P',\n                            'time': pick_time,\n                            'probability': p_probs[peak_idx]\n                        })\n\n                    for peak_idx in s_peaks:\n                        time_rel = peak_idx / sampling_rate\n                        pick_time = tr.stats.starttime + time_rel\n                        picks.append({\n                            'station_id': station_id,\n                            'phase_type': 'S',\n                            'time': pick_time,\n                            'probability': s_probs[peak_idx]\n                        })\n\n            except Exception as e:\n                continue\n\n    except ImportError:\n        # If torch is not available, use a simpler approach\n        print(\"PyTorch not available, using STA/LTA picker...\")\n        use_sta_lta(traces, station_id, picks)\n\ndef use_sta_lta(traces, station_id, picks):\n    \"\"\"Use STA/LTA as fallback if deep learning models fail.\"\"\"\n    for tr in traces:\n        try:\n            # Simple STA/LTA picker\n            data = tr.data\n            sampling_rate = tr.stats.sampling_rate\n\n            # STA/LTA parameters\n            sta_len = int(0.5 * sampling_rate)  # 0.5 seconds\n            lta_len = int(5.0 * sampling_rate)  # 5 seconds\n            thr_trigger = 4.0\n            thr_untrigger = 1.5\n\n            if len(data) > lta_len * 2:\n                # Calculate STA/LTA\n                sta = np.convolve(data**2, np.ones(sta_len)/sta_len, mode='same')\n                lta = np.convolve(data**2, np.ones(lta_len)/lta_len, mode='same')\n\n                ratio = sta / (lta + 1e-10)\n\n                # Find triggers\n                triggers = np.where(ratio > thr_trigger)[0]\n\n                for trigger_idx in triggers:\n                    # Simple peak finding after trigger\n                    window_start = trigger_idx\n                    window_end = min(trigger_idx + int(2.0 * sampling_rate), len(data))\n\n                    if window_end > window_start:\n                        window_data = data[window_start:window_end]\n                        peak_idx = window_start + np.argmax(np.abs(window_data))\n\n                        # Determine phase type based on frequency or just label as P\n                        picks.append({\n                            'station_id': station_id,\n                            'phase_type': 'P',\n                            'time': tr.stats.starttime + peak_idx / sampling_rate,\n                            'probability': ratio[peak_idx] / thr_trigger\n                        })\n\n        except Exception:\n            continue\n\ndef find_peaks_above_threshold(prob_array, threshold):\n    \"\"\"Find peaks in probability array above threshold.\"\"\"\n    from scipy.signal import find_peaks\n\n    peaks, _ = find_peaks(prob_array, height=threshold, distance=int(0.5 * 100))  # 0.5 second minimum distance\n    return peaks\n\ndef associate_phases(picks, station_dict):\n    \"\"\"Associate P and S picks to identify earthquake events.\"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nAssociating phase picks to identify events...\")\n\n    if not picks:\n        print(\"No picks to associate\")\n        return []\n\n    # Sort picks by time\n    picks_sorted = sorted(picks, key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    # Group picks into events using a time window\n    events = []\n    time_window = 30.0  # seconds\n\n    current_event = {\n        'picks': [],\n        'start_time': None,\n        'end_time': None\n    }\n\n    for pick in picks_sorted:\n        # Check if this pick belongs to the current event\n        if current_event['start_time'] is None:\n            # First pick\n            current_event['picks'] = [pick]\n            current_event['start_time'] = pick['time']\n            current_event['end_time'] = pick['time']\n        else:\n            # Check time difference\n            time_diff = (pick['time'] - current_event['end_time']).total_seconds()\n\n            if time_diff <= time_window:\n                # Add to current event\n                current_event['picks'].append(pick)\n                if pick['time'] > current_event['end_time']:\n                    current_event['end_time'] = pick['time']\n            else:\n                # Time gap too large, start new event\n                if len(current_event['picks']) >= 3:  # At least 3 picks\n                    events.append(current_event)\n\n                # Start new event\n                current_event = {\n                    'picks': [pick],\n                    'start_time': pick['time'],\n                    'end_time': pick['time']\n                }\n\n    # Don't forget the last event\n    if len(current_event['picks']) >= 3:\n        events.append(current_event)\n\n    # Process events to estimate event times\n    processed_events = []\n    for event in events:\n        # Count unique stations\n        unique_stations = set(p['station_id'] for p in event['picks'])\n\n        # Get P picks\n        p_picks = [p for p in event['picks'] if p['phase_type'] == 'P']\n\n        # Estimate event time as median of P picks\n        if p_picks:\n            p_times = sorted([p['time'] for p in p_picks])\n            median_idx = len(p_times) // 2\n\n            if len(p_times) % 2 == 0:\n                event_time = p_times[median_idx - 1] + (p_times[median_idx] - p_times[median_idx - 1]) / 2\n            else:\n                event_time = p_times[median_idx]\n        else:\n            # No P picks, use earliest pick\n            event_time = min(event['picks'], key=lambda x: x['time'])['time']\n\n        processed_events.append({\n            'time': event_time,\n            'picks': event['picks'],\n            'num_stations': len(unique_stations)\n        })\n\n    # Sort events by time\n    processed_events.sort(key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    print(f\"Identified {len(processed_events)} earthquake events\")\n    return processed_events\n\ndef write_results(events):\n    \"\"\"Write results to CSV file.\"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nWriting results to /root/results.csv...\")\n\n    results = []\n    for event in events:\n        # Convert to ISO format without timezone\n        time_str = event['time'].strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S.%f')[:-3]\n\n        results.append({\n            'time': time_str\n        })\n\n    df = pd.DataFrame(results)\n    df.to_csv('/root/results.csv', index=False)\n\n    print(f\"Wrote {len(results)} events to /root/results.csv\")\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Starting seismic phase association...\")\n\n    # Load data\n    st, stations_df = load_data()\n\n    # Create station dictionary\n    station_dict = create_station_dict(stations_df)\n\n    # Pick phases\n    picks = pick_phases(st)\n\n    if not picks:\n        print(\"No picks found. Exiting.\")\n        return\n\n    # Associate phases to events\n    events = associate_phases(picks, station_dict)\n\n    # Write results\n    write_results(events)\n\n    print(\"\\nDone!\")\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"}}
