km/s\nVS = VP / 1.75  # km/s\nEVENT_TIME_TOLERANCE = 5.0  # seconds\n\ndef load_data():\n    \"\"\"Load waveform and station data.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Loading waveform data...\")\n    st = read('/root/data/wave.mseed')\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(st)} traces\")\n\n    print(\"Loading station data...\")\n    stations_df = pd.read_csv('/root/data/stations.csv')\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(stations_df)} station channels\")\n\n    return st, stations_df\n\ndef create_station_dict(stations_df):\n    \"\"\"Create a dictionary mapping station names to their locations.\"\"\"\n    station_dict = {}\n    for _, row in stations_df.iterrows():\n        key = f\"{row['network']}.{row['station']}\"\n        if key not in station_dict:\n            station_dict[key] = {\n                'longitude': row['longitude'],\n                'latitude': row['latitude'],\n                'elevation_m': row['elevation_m']\n            }\n    return station_dict\n\ndef simple_picker(tr):\n    \"\"\"Simple energy-based picker for phase picking.\"\"\"\n    data = tr.data\n    sampling_rate = tr.stats.sampling_rate\n\n    # Filter data (simple bandpass-like effect)\n    # Remove DC component\n    data = data - np.mean(data)\n\n    # Calculate short-term average and long-term average\n    window_size = int(0.1 * sampling_rate)  # 0.1 second window\n    sta_len = int(0.5 * sampling_rate)  # 0.5 seconds\n    lta_len = int(5.0 * sampling_rate)  # 5 seconds\n\n    if len(data) < lta_len * 2:\n        return []\n\n    # Calculate squared data for better peak detection\n    data_sq = data ** 2\n\n    # Calculate STA and LTA\n    sta = np.convolve(data_sq, np.ones(sta_len) / sta_len, mode='same')\n    lta = np.convolve(data_sq, np.ones(lta_len) / lta_len, mode='same')\n\n    # Avoid division by zero\n    lta = np.maximum(lta, 1e-10)\n\n    # Calculate ratio\n    ratio = sta / lta\n\n    # Find triggers\n    threshold = 3.0\n    triggers = np.where(ratio > threshold)[0]\n\n    picks = []\n    for trigger in triggers:\n        # Find peak within a window after trigger\n        window_start = trigger\n        window_end = min(trigger + int(2.0 * sampling_rate), len(data))\n\n        if window_end > window_start:\n            window_data = data[window_start:window_end]\n            if len(window_data) > 0:\n                peak_idx = window_start + np.argmax(np.abs(window_data))\n\n                # Estimate probability based on ratio\n                prob = min(ratio[peak_idx] / 10.0, 1.0)\n\n                picks.append({\n                    'sample_idx': peak_idx,\n                    'probability': prob\n                })\n\n    return picks\n\ndef pick_phases(st):\n    \"\"\"Pick P and S waves using simple energy-based picker.\"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nPicking P and S waves using energy-based picker...\")\n\n    picks = []\n\n    # Process each trace\n    for tr in st:\n        try:\n            # Get trace metadata\n            stats = tr.stats\n            station_id = f\"{stats.network}.{stats.station}\"\n\n            # Get picks using simple picker\n            trace_picks = simple_picker(tr)\n\n            # Convert to absolute times and add to picks list\n            for pick in trace_picks:\n                time_rel = pick['sample_idx'] / stats.sampling_rate\n                pick_time = stats.starttime + time_rel\n                picks.append({\n                    'station_id': station_id,\n                    'phase_type': 'P',  # Label all as P for now\n                    'time': pick_time,\n                    'probability': pick['probability']\n                })\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Warning: Error processing trace: {e}\")\n            continue\n\n    print(f\"Found {len(picks)} phase picks\")\n    return picks\n\ndef associate_phases_gamma(picks, station_dict):\n    \"\"\"\n    Associate phase picks using a simplified GaMMA-like algorithm.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nAssociating phase picks to identify events...\")\n\n    if not picks:\n        print(\"No picks to associate\")\n        return []\n\n    # Sort picks by time\n    picks_sorted = sorted(picks, key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    # Group picks into events using time window\n    events = []\n    time_window = 30.0  # seconds - events are within this window\n\n    current_event_picks = []\n\n    for pick in picks_sorted:\n        # Check if this pick belongs to the current event\n        if not current_event_picks:\n            # First pick\n            current_event_picks = [pick]\n        else:\n            # Check time difference from first pick\n            time_diff = (pick['time'] - current_event_picks[0]['time']).total_seconds()\n\n            if time_diff <= time_window:\n                # Add to current event\n                current_event_picks.append(pick)\n            else:\n                # Time gap too large, finalize current event\n                if len(current_event_picks) >= 3:  # At least 3 picks\n                    events.append(current_event_picks)\n\n                # Start new event\n                current_event_picks = [pick]\n\n    # Don't forget the last event\n    if len(current_event_picks) >= 3:\n        events.append(current_event_picks)\n\n    # Process events to estimate event times\n    processed_events = []\n    for event_picks in events:\n        # Count unique stations\n        unique_stations = set(p['station_id'] for p in event_picks)\n\n        # Only keep events with multiple stations\n        if len(unique_stations) >= 3:\n            # Estimate event time as median of all picks\n            all_times = sorted([p['time'] for p in event_picks])\n            median_idx = len(all_times) // 2\n\n            if len(all_times) % 2 == 0:\n                event_time = all_times[median_idx - 1] + (all_times[median_idx] - all_times[median_idx - 1]) / 2\n            else:\n                event_time = all_times[median_idx]\n\n            processed_events.append({\n                'time': event_time,\n                'num_stations': len(unique_stations)\n            })\n\n    # Sort events by time\n    processed_events.sort(key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    print(f\"Identified {len(processed_events)} earthquake events\")\n    return processed_events\n\ndef associate_phases_simple(picks, station_dict):\n    \"\"\"\n    Simple association based on time clustering.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nAssociating phase picks to identify events...\")\n\n    if not picks:\n        print(\"No picks to associate\")\n        return []\n\n    # Sort picks by time\n    picks_sorted = sorted(picks, key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    # Use a sliding window to group picks\n    events = []\n    window_size = 20.0  # seconds\n    min_picks = 5\n\n    i = 0\n    while i < len(picks_sorted):\n        # km/s\nVS = VP / 1.75  # km/s\nEVENT_TIME_TOLERANCE = 5.0  # seconds\n\ndef load_data():\n    \"\"\"Load waveform and station data.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Loading waveform data...\")\n    st = read('/root/data/wave.mseed')\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(st)} traces\")\n\n    print(\"Loading station data...\")\n    stations_df = pd.read_csv('/root/data/stations.csv')\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(stations_df)} station channels\")\n\n    return st, stations_df\n\ndef create_station_dict(stations_df):\n    \"\"\"Create a dictionary mapping station names to their locations.\"\"\"\n    station_dict = {}\n    for _, row in stations_df.iterrows():\n        key = f\"{row['network']}.{row['station']}\"\n        if key not in station_dict:\n            station_dict[key] = {\n                'longitude': row['longitude'],\n                'latitude': row['latitude'],\n                'elevation_m': row['elevation_m']\n            }\n    return station_dict\n\ndef simple_picker(tr):\n    \"\"\"Simple energy-based picker for phase picking.\"\"\"\n    data = tr.data\n    sampling_rate = tr.stats.sampling_rate\n\n    # Filter data (simple bandpass-like effect)\n    # Remove DC component\n    data = data - np.mean(data)\n\n    # Calculate short-term average and long-term average\n    window_size = int(0.1 * sampling_rate)  # 0.1 second window\n    sta_len = int(0.5 * sampling_rate)  # 0.5 seconds\n    lta_len = int(5.0 * sampling_rate)  # 5 seconds\n\n    if len(data) < lta_len * 2:\n        return []\n\n    # Calculate squared data for better peak detection\n    data_sq = data ** 2\n\n    # Calculate STA and LTA\n    sta = np.convolve(data_sq, np.ones(sta_len) / sta_len, mode='same')\n    lta = np.convolve(data_sq, np.ones(lta_len) / lta_len, mode='same')\n\n    # Avoid division by zero\n    lta = np.maximum(lta, 1e-10)\n\n    # Calculate ratio\n    ratio = sta / lta\n\n    # Find triggers\n    threshold = 3.0\n    triggers = np.where(ratio > threshold)[0]\n\n    picks = []\n    for trigger in triggers:\n        # Find peak within a window after trigger\n        window_start = trigger\n        window_end = min(trigger + int(2.0 * sampling_rate), len(data))\n\n        if window_end > window_start:\n            window_data = data[window_start:window_end]\n            if len(window_data) > 0:\n                peak_idx = window_start + np.argmax(np.abs(window_data))\n\n                # Estimate probability based on ratio\n                prob = min(ratio[peak_idx] / 10.0, 1.0)\n\n                picks.append({\n                    'sample_idx': peak_idx,\n                    'probability': prob\n                })\n\n    return picks\n\ndef pick_phases(st):\n    \"\"\"Pick P and S waves using simple energy-based picker.\"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nPicking P and S waves using energy-based picker...\")\n\n    picks = []\n\n    # Process each trace\n    for tr in st:\n        try:\n            # Get trace metadata\n            stats = tr.stats\n            station_id = f\"{stats.network}.{stats.station}\"\n\n            # Get picks using simple picker\n            trace_picks = simple_picker(tr)\n\n            # Convert to absolute times and add to picks list\n            for pick in trace_picks:\n                time_rel = pick['sample_idx'] / stats.sampling_rate\n                pick_time = stats.starttime + time_rel\n                picks.append({\n                    'station_id': station_id,\n                    'phase_type': 'P',  # Label all as P for now\n                    'time': pick_time,\n                    'probability': pick['probability']\n                })\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Warning: Error processing trace: {e}\")\n            continue\n\n    print(f\"Found {len(picks)} phase picks\")\n    return picks\n\ndef associate_phases_gamma(picks, station_dict):\n    \"\"\"\n    Associate phase picks using a simplified GaMMA-like algorithm.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nAssociating phase picks to identify events...\")\n\n    if not picks:\n        print(\"No picks to associate\")\n        return []\n\n    # Sort picks by time\n    picks_sorted = sorted(picks, key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    # Group picks into events using time window\n    events = []\n    time_window = 30.0  # seconds - events are within this window\n\n    current_event_picks = []\n\n    for pick in picks_sorted:\n        # Check if this pick belongs to the current event\n        if not current_event_picks:\n            # First pick\n            current_event_picks = [pick]\n        else:\n            # Check time difference from first pick\n            time_diff = (pick['time'] - current_event_picks[0]['time']).total_seconds()\n\n            if time_diff <= time_window:\n                # Add to current event\n                current_event_picks.append(pick)\n            else:\n                # Time gap too large, finalize current event\n                if len(current_event_picks) >= 3:  # At least 3 picks\n                    events.append(current_event_picks)\n\n                # Start new event\n                current_event_picks = [pick]\n\n    # Don't forget the last event\n    if len(current_event_picks) >= 3:\n        events.append(current_event_picks)\n\n    # Process events to estimate event times\n    processed_events = []\n    for event_picks in events:\n        # Count unique stations\n        unique_stations = set(p['station_id'] for p in event_picks)\n\n        # Only keep events with multiple stations\n        if len(unique_stations) >= 3:\n            # Estimate event time as median of all picks\n            all_times = sorted([p['time'] for p in event_picks])\n            median_idx = len(all_times) // 2\n\n            if len(all_times) % 2 == 0:\n                event_time = all_times[median_idx - 1] + (all_times[median_idx] - all_times[median_idx - 1]) / 2\n            else:\n                event_time = all_times[median_idx]\n\n            processed_events.append({\n                'time': event_time,\n                'num_stations': len(unique_stations)\n            })\n\n    # Sort events by time\n    processed_events.sort(key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    print(f\"Identified {len(processed_events)} earthquake events\")\n    return processed_events\n\ndef associate_phases_simple(picks, station_dict):\n    \"\"\"\n    Simple association based on time clustering.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nAssociating phase picks to identify events...\")\n\n    if not picks:\n        print(\"No picks to associate\")\n        return []\n\n    # Sort picks by time\n    picks_sorted = sorted(picks, key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    # Use a sliding window to group picks\n    events = []\n    window_size = 20.0  # seconds\n    min_picks = 5\n\n    i = 0\n    while i < len(picks_sorted):\n        # 0.1 second window\n    sta_len = int(0.5 * sampling_rate)  # 0.5 seconds\n    lta_len = int(5.0 * sampling_rate)  # 5 seconds\n\n    if len(data) < lta_len * 2:\n        return []\n\n    # Calculate squared data for better peak detection\n    data_sq = data ** 2\n\n    # Calculate STA and LTA\n    sta = np.convolve(data_sq, np.ones(sta_len) / sta_len, mode='same')\n    lta = np.convolve(data_sq, np.ones(lta_len) / lta_len, mode='same')\n\n    # Avoid division by zero\n    lta = np.maximum(lta, 1e-10)\n\n    # Calculate ratio\n    ratio = sta / lta\n\n    # Find triggers\n    threshold = 3.0\n    triggers = np.where(ratio > threshold)[0]\n\n    picks = []\n    for trigger in triggers:\n        # Find peak within a window after trigger\n        window_start = trigger\n        window_end = min(trigger + int(2.0 * sampling_rate), len(data))\n\n        if window_end > window_start:\n            window_data = data[window_start:window_end]\n            if len(window_data) > 0:\n                peak_idx = window_start + np.argmax(np.abs(window_data))\n\n                # Convert to absolute times and add to picks list\n            for pick in trace_picks:\n                time_rel = pick['sample_idx'] / stats.sampling_rate\n                pick_time = stats.starttime + time_rel\n                picks.append({\n                    'station_id': station_id,\n                    'phase_type': 'P',  # Sort picks by time\n    picks_sorted = sorted(picks, key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    # Group picks into events using time window\n    events = []\n    time_window = 30.0  # seconds - events are within this window\n\n    current_event_picks = []\n\n    for pick in picks_sorted:\n        # Check if this pick belongs to the current event\n        if not current_event_picks:\n            # First pick\n            current_event_picks = [pick]\n        else:\n            # Check time difference from first pick\n            time_diff = (pick['time'] - current_event_picks[0]['time']).total_seconds()\n\n            if time_diff <= time_window:\n                # Add to current event\n                current_event_picks.append(pick)\n            else:\n                # Time gap too large, finalize current event\n                if len(current_event_picks) >= 3:  # At least 3 picks\n                    events.append(current_event_picks)\n\n                # Start new event\n                current_event_picks = [pick]\n\n    # Don't forget the last event\n    if len(current_event_picks) >= 3:\n        events.append(current_event_picks)\n\n    # Process events to estimate event times\n    processed_events = []\n    for event_picks in events:\n        # Count unique stations\n        unique_stations = set(p['station_id'] for p in event_picks)\n\n        # Only keep events with multiple stations\n        if len(unique_stations) >= 3:\n            # Estimate event time as median of all picks\n            all_times = sorted([p['time'] for p in event_picks])\n            median_idx = len(all_times) // 2\n\n            if len(all_times) % 2 == 0:\n                event_time = all_times[median_idx - 1] + (all_times[median_idx] - all_times[median_idx - 1]) / 2\n            else:\n                event_time = all_times[median_idx]\n\n            processed_events.append({\n                'time': event_time,\n                'num_stations': len(unique_stations)\n            })\n\n    # Sort events by time\n    processed_events.sort(key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    print(f\"Identified {len(processed_events)} earthquake events\")\n    return processed_events\n\ndef associate_phases_simple(picks, station_dict):\n    \"\"\"\n    Simple association based on time clustering.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nAssociating phase picks to identify events...\")\n\n    if not picks:\n        print(\"No picks to associate\")\n        return []\n\n    # Sort picks by time\n    picks_sorted = sorted(picks, key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    # Use a sliding window to group picks\n    events = []\n    window_size = 20.0  # seconds\n    min_picks = 5\n\n    i = 0\n    while i < len(picks_sorted):\n        # Start a new potential event\n        event_start_time = picks_sorted[i]['time']\n        event_picks = [picks_sorted[i]]\n\n        # Add picks within window\n        j = i + 1\n        while j < len(picks_sorted):\n            time_diff = (picks_sorted[j]['time'] - event_start_time).total_seconds()\n            if time_diff <= window_size:\n                event_picks.append(picks_sorted[j])\n                j += 1\n            else:\n                break\n\n        # Check if we have enough picks and stations\n        if len(event_picks) >= min_picks:\n            unique_stations = set(p['station_id'] for p in event_picks)\n\n            # Require at least 3 different stations\n            if len(unique_stations) >= 3:\n                # Estimate event time as median of earliest picks\n                event_picks.sort(key=lambda x: x['time'])\n                event_time = event_picks[len(event_picks) // 2]['time']\n\n                events.append({\n                    'time': event_time,\n                    'num_stations': len(unique_stations)\n                })\n\n        # Move to next event (skip overlapping)\n        if j > i + 1:\n            i = j\n        else:\n            i += 1\n\n    # Sort events by time\n    events.sort(key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    print(f\"Identified {len(events)} earthquake events\")\n    return events\n\ndef write_results(events):\n    \"\"\"Write results to CSV file.\"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nWriting results to /root/results.csv...\")\n\n    results = []\n    for event in events:\n        # km/s\nVS = VP / 1.75  # km/s\nEVENT_TIME_TOLERANCE = 5.0  # seconds\n\ndef load_data():\n    \"\"\"Load waveform and station data.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Loading waveform data...\")\n    st = read('/root/data/wave.mseed')\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(st)} traces\")\n\n    print(\"Loading station data...\")\n    stations_df = pd.read_csv('/root/data/stations.csv')\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(stations_df)} station channels\")\n\n    return st, stations_df\n\ndef create_station_dict(stations_df):\n    \"\"\"Create a dictionary mapping station names to their locations.\"\"\"\n    station_dict = {}\n    for _, row in stations_df.iterrows():\n        key = f\"{row['network']}.{row['station']}\"\n        if key not in station_dict:\n            station_dict[key] = {\n                'longitude': row['longitude'],\n                'latitude': row['latitude'],\n                'elevation_m': row['elevation_m']\n            }\n    return station_dict\n\ndef simple_picker(tr):\n    \"\"\"Simple energy-based picker for phase picking.\"\"\"\n    data = tr.data\n    sampling_rate = tr.stats.sampling_rate\n\n    # Filter data (simple bandpass-like effect)\n    # Remove DC component\n    data = data - np.mean(data)\n\n    # Calculate short-term average and long-term average\n    window_size = int(0.1 * sampling_rate)  # 0.1 second window\n    sta_len = int(0.5 * sampling_rate)  # 0.5 seconds\n    lta_len = int(5.0 * sampling_rate)  # 5 seconds\n\n    if len(data) < lta_len * 2:\n        return []\n\n    # Calculate squared data for better peak detection\n    data_sq = data ** 2\n\n    # Calculate STA and LTA\n    sta = np.convolve(data_sq, np.ones(sta_len) / sta_len, mode='same')\n    lta = np.convolve(data_sq, np.ones(lta_len) / lta_len, mode='same')\n\n    # Avoid division by zero\n    lta = np.maximum(lta, 1e-10)\n\n    # Calculate ratio\n    ratio = sta / lta\n\n    # Find triggers with higher threshold to reduce noise\n    threshold = 10.0\n    triggers = np.where(ratio > threshold)[0]\n\n    # Remove triggers that are too close together\n    min_distance = int(1.0 * sampling_rate)  # 1 second minimum distance between picks\n    if len(triggers) > 0:\n        filtered_triggers = [triggers[0]]\n        for trigger in triggers[1:]:\n            if trigger - filtered_triggers[-1] >= min_distance:\n                filtered_triggers.append(trigger)\n        triggers = np.array(filtered_triggers)\n\n    picks = []\n    for trigger in triggers:\n        # Find peak within a window after trigger\n        window_start = trigger\n        window_end = min(trigger + int(2.0 * sampling_rate), len(data))\n\n        if window_end > window_start:\n            window_data = data[window_start:window_end]\n            if len(window_data) > 0:\n                peak_idx = window_start + np.argmax(np.abs(window_data))\n\n                # Estimate probability based on ratio\n                prob = min(ratio[peak_idx] / 10.0, 1.0)\n\n                picks.append({\n                    'sample_idx': peak_idx,\n                    'probability': prob\n                })\n\n    return picks\n\ndef pick_phases(st):\n    \"\"\"Pick P and S waves using simple energy-based picker.\"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nPicking P and S waves using energy-based picker...\")\n\n    picks = []\n\n    # Process each trace\n    for tr in st:\n        try:\n            # Get trace metadata\n            stats = tr.stats\n            station_id = f\"{stats.network}.{stats.station}\"\n\n            # Get picks using simple picker\n            trace_picks = simple_picker(tr)\n\n            # Convert to absolute times and add to picks list\n            for pick in trace_picks:\n                time_rel = pick['sample_idx'] / stats.sampling_rate\n                pick_time = stats.starttime + time_rel\n                picks.append({\n                    'station_id': station_id,\n                    'phase_type': 'P',  # Label all as P for now\n                    'time': pick_time,\n                    'probability': pick['probability']\n                })\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Warning: Error processing trace: {e}\")\n            continue\n\n    print(f\"Found {len(picks)} phase picks\")\n    return picks\n\ndef associate_phases_gamma(picks, station_dict):\n    \"\"\"\n    Associate phase picks using a simplified GaMMA-like algorithm.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nAssociating phase picks to identify events...\")\n\n    if not picks:\n        print(\"No picks to associate\")\n        return []\n\n    # Sort picks by time\n    picks_sorted = sorted(picks, key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    # Group picks into events using time window\n    events = []\n    time_window = 30.0  # seconds - events are within this window\n\n    current_event_picks = []\n\n    for pick in picks_sorted:\n        # Check if this pick belongs to the current event\n        if not current_event_picks:\n            # First pick\n            current_event_picks = [pick]\n        else:\n            # Check time difference from first pick\n            time_diff = (pick['time'] - current_event_picks[0]['time']).total_seconds()\n\n            if time_diff <= time_window:\n                # Add to current event\n                current_event_picks.append(pick)\n            else:\n                # Time gap too large, finalize current event\n                if len(current_event_picks) >= 3:  # At least 3 picks\n                    events.append(current_event_picks)\n\n                # Start new event\n                current_event_picks = [pick]\n\n    # Don't forget the last event\n    if len(current_event_picks) >= 3:\n        events.append(current_event_picks)\n\n    # Process events to estimate event times\n    processed_events = []\n    for event_picks in events:\n        # Count unique stations\n        unique_stations = set(p['station_id'] for p in event_picks)\n\n        # Only keep events with multiple stations\n        if len(unique_stations) >= 3:\n            # Estimate event time as median of all picks\n            all_times = sorted([p['time'] for p in event_picks])\n            median_idx = len(all_times) // 2\n\n            if len(all_times) % 2 == 0:\n                event_time = all_times[median_idx - 1] + (all_times[median_idx] - all_times[median_idx - 1]) / 2\n            else:\n                event_time = all_times[median_idx]\n\n            processed_events.append({\n                'time': event_time,\n                'num_stations': len(unique_stations)\n            })\n\n    # Sort events by time\n    processed_events.sort(key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    print(f\"Identified {len(processed_events)} earthquake events\")\n    return processed_events\n\ndef associate_phases_simple(picks, station_dict):\n    \"\"\"\n    Simple association based on time clustering.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nAssociating phase picks to identify events...\")\n\n    if not picks:\n        print(\"No picks to associate\")\n        return []\n\n    # Sort picks by time\n    picks_sorted = sorted(picks, key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    # Use a sliding window to group picks\n    events = []\n    window_size = 20.0  # seconds\n    min_picks = 5\n\n    i = 0\n    while i < len(picks_sorted):\n        # Start a new potential event\n        event_start_time = picks_sorted[i]['time']\n        event_picks = [picks_sorted[i]]\n\n        # Add picks within window\n        j = i + 1\n        while j < len(picks_sorted):\n            time_diff = (picks_sorted[j]['time'] - event_start_time).total_seconds()\n            if time_diff <= window_size:\n                event_picks.append(picks_sorted[j])\n                j += 1\n            else:\n                break\n\n        # Check if we have enough picks and stations\n        if len(event_picks) >= min_picks:\n            unique_stations = set(p['station_id'] for p in event_picks)\n\n            # Require at least 3 different stations\n            if len(unique_stations) >= 3:\n                # Estimate event time as median of earliest picks\n                event_picks.sort(key=lambda x: x['time'])\n                event_time = event_picks[len(event_picks) // 2]['time']\n\n                events.append({\n                    'time': event_time,\n                    'num_stations': len(unique_stations)\n                })\n\n        # Move to next event (skip overlapping)\n        if j > i + 1:\n            i = j\n        else:\n            i += 1\n\n    # Sort events by time\n    events.sort(key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    print(f\"Identified {len(events)} earthquake events\")\n    return events\n\ndef write_results(events):\n    \"\"\"Write results to CSV file.\"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nWriting results to /root/results.csv...\")\n\n    results = []\n    for event in events:\n        # Label all as P for now\n                    'time': pick_time,\n                    'probability': pick['probability']\n                })\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Warning: Error processing trace: {e}\")\n            continue\n\n    print(f\"Found {len(picks)} phase picks\")\n    return picks\n\ndef associate_phases_gamma(picks, station_dict):\n    \"\"\"\n    Associate phase picks using a simplified GaMMA-like algorithm.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nAssociating phase picks to identify events...\")\n\n    if not picks:\n        print(\"No picks to associate\")\n        return []\n\n    # Sort picks by time\n    picks_sorted = sorted(picks, key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    # Group picks into events using time window\n    events = []\n    time_window = 30.0  # seconds - events are within this window\n\n    current_event_picks = []\n\n    for pick in picks_sorted:\n        # Check if this pick belongs to the current event\n        if not current_event_picks:\n            # First pick\n            current_event_picks = [pick]\n        else:\n            # Check time difference from first pick\n            time_diff = (pick['time'] - current_event_picks[0]['time']).total_seconds()\n\n            if time_diff <= time_window:\n                # Add to current event\n                current_event_picks.append(pick)\n            else:\n                # Time gap too large, finalize current event\n                if len(current_event_picks) >= 3:  # At least 3 picks\n                    events.append(current_event_picks)\n\n                # Start new event\n                current_event_picks = [pick]\n\n    # Don't forget the last event\n    if len(current_event_picks) >= 3:\n        events.append(current_event_picks)\n\n    # Process events to estimate event times\n    processed_events = []\n    for event_picks in events:\n        # Count unique stations\n        unique_stations = set(p['station_id'] for p in event_picks)\n\n        # Only keep events with multiple stations\n        if len(unique_stations) >= 3:\n            # Estimate event time as median of all picks\n            all_times = sorted([p['time'] for p in event_picks])\n            median_idx = len(all_times) // 2\n\n            if len(all_times) % 2 == 0:\n                event_time = all_times[median_idx - 1] + (all_times[median_idx] - all_times[median_idx - 1]) / 2\n            else:\n                event_time = all_times[median_idx]\n\n            processed_events.append({\n                'time': event_time,\n                'num_stations': len(unique_stations)\n            })\n\n    # Sort events by time\n    processed_events.sort(key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    print(f\"Identified {len(processed_events)} earthquake events\")\n    return processed_events\n\ndef associate_phases_simple(picks, station_dict):\n    \"\"\"\n    Simple association based on time clustering.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nAssociating phase picks to identify events...\")\n\n    if not picks:\n        print(\"No picks to associate\")\n        return []\n\n    # Sort picks by time\n    picks_sorted = sorted(picks, key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    # Use a sliding window to group picks\n    events = []\n    window_size = 20.0  # seconds\n    min_picks = 5\n\n    i = 0\n    while i < len(picks_sorted):\n        # Start a new potential event\n        event_start_time = picks_sorted[i]['time']\n        event_picks = [picks_sorted[i]]\n\n        # Add picks within window\n        j = i + 1\n        while j < len(picks_sorted):\n            time_diff = (picks_sorted[j]['time'] - event_start_time).total_seconds()\n            if time_diff <= window_size:\n                event_picks.append(picks_sorted[j])\n                j += 1\n            else:\n                break\n\n        # Check if we have enough picks and stations\n        if len(event_picks) >= min_picks:\n            unique_stations = set(p['station_id'] for p in event_picks)\n\n            # Require at least 3 different stations\n            if len(unique_stations) >= 3:\n                # Estimate event time as median of earliest picks\n                event_picks.sort(key=lambda x: x['time'])\n                event_time = event_picks[len(event_picks) // 2]['time']\n\n                events.append({\n                    'time': event_time,\n                    'num_stations': len(unique_stations)\n                })\n\n        # Move to next event (skip overlapping)\n        if j > i + 1:\n            i = j\n        else:\n            i += 1\n\n    # Sort events by time\n    events.sort(key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    print(f\"Identified {len(events)} earthquake events\")\n    return events\n\ndef write_results(events):\n    \"\"\"Write results to CSV file.\"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nWriting results to /root/results.csv...\")\n\n    results = []\n    for event in events:\n        # Convert to ISO format without timezone\n        time_str = event['time'].strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S.%f')[:-3]\n\n        results.append({\n            'time': time_str\n        })\n\n    df = pd.DataFrame(results)\n    df.to_csv('/root/results.csv', index=False)\n\n    print(f\"Wrote {len(results)} events to /root/results.csv\")\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Starting seismic phase association...\")\n\n    # Load data\n    st, stations_df = load_data()\n\n    # Create station dictionary\n    station_dict = create_station_dict(stations_df)\n\n    # Pick phases\n    picks = pick_phases(st)\n\n    if not picks:\n        print(\"No picks found. Exiting.\")\n        return\n\n    # Associate phases to events\n    events = associate_phases_simple(picks, station_dict)\n\n    # Write results\n    write_results(events)\n\n    print(\"\\nDone!\")\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":227,"oldLines":8,"newStart":227,"newLines":8,"lines":[" ","     # Use a sliding window to group picks","     events = []","-    window_size = 20.0  # seconds","-    min_picks = 5","+    window_size = 30.0  # seconds","+    min_picks = 3  # Reduced from 5"," ","     i = 0","     while i < len(picks_sorted):"]},{"oldStart":250,"oldLines":8,"newStart":250,"newLines":8,"lines":["         if len(event_picks) >= min_picks:","             unique_stations = set(p['station_id'] for p in event_picks)"," ","-            # Require at least 3 different stations","-            if len(unique_stations) >= 3:","+            # Require at least 2 different stations (reduced from 3)","+            if len(unique_stations) >= 2:","                 # Estimate event time as median of earliest picks","                 event_picks.sort(key=lambda x: x['time'])","                 event_time = event_picks[len(event_picks) // 2]['time']"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

km/s\nVS = VP / 1.75  # km/s\nEVENT_TIME_TOLERANCE = 5.0  # seconds\n\ndef load_data():\n    \"\"\"Load waveform and station data.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Loading waveform data...\")\n    st = read('/root/data/wave.mseed')\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(st)} traces\")\n\n    print(\"Loading station data...\")\n    stations_df = pd.read_csv('/root/data/stations.csv')\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(stations_df)} station channels\")\n\n    return st, stations_df\n\ndef create_station_dict(stations_df):\n    \"\"\"Create a dictionary mapping station names to their locations.\"\"\"\n    station_dict = {}\n    for _, row in stations_df.iterrows():\n        key = f\"{row['network']}.{row['station']}\"\n        if key not in station_dict:\n            station_dict[key] = {\n                'longitude': row['longitude'],\n                'latitude': row['latitude'],\n                'elevation_m': row['elevation_m']\n            }\n    return station_dict\n\ndef simple_picker(tr):\n    \"\"\"Simple energy-based picker for phase picking.\"\"\"\n    data = tr.data\n    sampling_rate = tr.stats.sampling_rate\n\n    # Filter data (simple bandpass-like effect)\n    # Remove DC component\n    data = data - np.mean(data)\n\n    # Calculate short-term average and long-term average\n    window_size = int(0.1 * sampling_rate)  # 0.1 second window\n    sta_len = int(0.5 * sampling_rate)  # 0.5 seconds\n    lta_len = int(5.0 * sampling_rate)  # 5 seconds\n\n    if len(data) < lta_len * 2:\n        return []\n\n    # Calculate squared data for better peak detection\n    data_sq = data ** 2\n\n    # Calculate STA and LTA\n    sta = np.convolve(data_sq, np.ones(sta_len) / sta_len, mode='same')\n    lta = np.convolve(data_sq, np.ones(lta_len) / lta_len, mode='same')\n\n    # Avoid division by zero\n    lta = np.maximum(lta, 1e-10)\n\n    # Calculate ratio\n    ratio = sta / lta\n\n    # Find triggers with moderate threshold\n    threshold = 6.0\n    triggers = np.where(ratio > threshold)[0]\n\n    # Remove triggers that are too close together\n    min_distance = int(2.0 * sampling_rate)  # 2 seconds minimum distance between picks\n    if len(triggers) > 0:\n        filtered_triggers = [triggers[0]]\n        for trigger in triggers[1:]:\n            if trigger - filtered_triggers[-1] >= min_distance:\n                filtered_triggers.append(trigger)\n        triggers = np.array(filtered_triggers)\n\n    picks = []\n    for trigger in triggers:\n        # Find peak within a window after trigger\n        window_start = trigger\n        window_end = min(trigger + int(2.0 * sampling_rate), len(data))\n\n        if window_end > window_start:\n            window_data = data[window_start:window_end]\n            if len(window_data) > 0:\n                peak_idx = window_start + np.argmax(np.abs(window_data))\n\n                # Estimate probability based on ratio\n                prob = min(ratio[peak_idx] / 10.0, 1.0)\n\n                picks.append({\n                    'sample_idx': peak_idx,\n                    'probability': prob\n                })\n\n    return picks\n\ndef pick_phases(st):\n    \"\"\"Pick P and S waves using simple energy-based picker.\"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nPicking P and S waves using energy-based picker...\")\n\n    picks = []\n\n    # Process each trace\n    for tr in st:\n        try:\n            # Get trace metadata\n            stats = tr.stats\n            station_id = f\"{stats.network}.{stats.station}\"\n\n            # Get picks using simple picker\n            trace_picks = simple_picker(tr)\n\n            # Convert to absolute times and add to picks list\n            for pick in trace_picks:\n                time_rel = pick['sample_idx'] / stats.sampling_rate\n                pick_time = stats.starttime + time_rel\n                picks.append({\n                    'station_id': station_id,\n                    'phase_type': 'P',  # Label all as P for now\n                    'time': pick_time,\n                    'probability': pick['probability']\n                })\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Warning: Error processing trace: {e}\")\n            continue\n\n    print(f\"Found {len(picks)} phase picks\")\n    return picks\n\ndef associate_phases_gamma(picks, station_dict):\n    \"\"\"\n    Associate phase picks using a simplified GaMMA-like algorithm.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nAssociating phase picks to identify events...\")\n\n    if not picks:\n        print(\"No picks to associate\")\n        return []\n\n    # Sort picks by time\n    picks_sorted = sorted(picks, key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    # Group picks into events using time window\n    events = []\n    time_window = 30.0  # seconds - events are within this window\n\n    current_event_picks = []\n\n    for pick in picks_sorted:\n        # Check if this pick belongs to the current event\n        if not current_event_picks:\n            # First pick\n            current_event_picks = [pick]\n        else:\n            # Check time difference from first pick\n            time_diff = (pick['time'] - current_event_picks[0]['time']).total_seconds()\n\n            if time_diff <= time_window:\n                # Add to current event\n                current_event_picks.append(pick)\n            else:\n                # Time gap too large, finalize current event\n                if len(current_event_picks) >= 3:  # At least 3 picks\n                    events.append(current_event_picks)\n\n                # Start new event\n                current_event_picks = [pick]\n\n    # Don't forget the last event\n    if len(current_event_picks) >= 3:\n        events.append(current_event_picks)\n\n    # Process events to estimate event times\n    processed_events = []\n    for event_picks in events:\n        # Count unique stations\n        unique_stations = set(p['station_id'] for p in event_picks)\n\n        # Only keep events with multiple stations\n        if len(unique_stations) >= 3:\n            # Estimate event time as median of all picks\n            all_times = sorted([p['time'] for p in event_picks])\n            median_idx = len(all_times) // 2\n\n            if len(all_times) % 2 == 0:\n                event_time = all_times[median_idx - 1] + (all_times[median_idx] - all_times[median_idx - 1]) / 2\n            else:\n                event_time = all_times[median_idx]\n\n            processed_events.append({\n                'time': event_time,\n                'num_stations': len(unique_stations)\n            })\n\n    # Sort events by time\n    processed_events.sort(key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    print(f\"Identified {len(processed_events)} earthquake events\")\n    return processed_events\n\ndef associate_phases_simple(picks, station_dict):\n    \"\"\"\n    Simple association based on time clustering.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nAssociating phase picks to identify events...\")\n\n    if not picks:\n        print(\"No picks to associate\")\n        return []\n\n    # Sort picks by time\n    picks_sorted = sorted(picks, key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    # Use a sliding window to group picks\n    events = []\n    window_size = 30.0  # seconds\n    min_picks = 3  # Reduced from 5\n\n    i = 0\n    while i < len(picks_sorted):\n        # Start a new potential event\n        event_start_time = picks_sorted[i]['time']\n        event_picks = [picks_sorted[i]]\n\n        # Add picks within window\n        j = i + 1\n        while j < len(picks_sorted):\n            time_diff = (picks_sorted[j]['time'] - event_start_time).total_seconds()\n            if time_diff <= window_size:\n                event_picks.append(picks_sorted[j])\n                j += 1\n            else:\n                break\n\n        # Check if we have enough picks and stations\n        if len(event_picks) >= min_picks:\n            unique_stations = set(p['station_id'] for p in event_picks)\n\n            # Require at least 2 different stations (reduced from 3)\n            if len(unique_stations) >= 2:\n                # Estimate event time as median of earliest picks\n                event_picks.sort(key=lambda x: x['time'])\n                event_time = event_picks[len(event_picks) // 2]['time']\n\n                events.append({\n                    'time': event_time,\n                    'num_stations': len(unique_stations)\n                })\n\n        # Move to next event (skip overlapping)\n        if j > i + 1:\n            i = j\n        else:\n            i += 1\n\n    # Sort events by time\n    events.sort(key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    print(f\"Identified {len(events)} earthquake events\")\n    return events\n\ndef write_results(events):\n    \"\"\"Write results to CSV file.\"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nWriting results to /root/results.csv...\")\n\n    results = []\n    for event in events:\n        # km/s\nVS = VP / 1.75  # km/s\nEVENT_TIME_TOLERANCE = 5.0  # seconds\n\ndef load_data():\n    \"\"\"Load waveform and station data.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Loading waveform data...\")\n    st = read('/root/data/wave.mseed')\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(st)} traces\")\n\n    print(\"Loading station data...\")\n    stations_df = pd.read_csv('/root/data/stations.csv')\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(stations_df)} station channels\")\n\n    return st, stations_df\n\ndef create_station_dict(stations_df):\n    \"\"\"Create a dictionary mapping station names to their locations.\"\"\"\n    station_dict = {}\n    for _, row in stations_df.iterrows():\n        key = f\"{row['network']}.{row['station']}\"\n        if key not in station_dict:\n            station_dict[key] = {\n                'longitude': row['longitude'],\n                'latitude': row['latitude'],\n                'elevation_m': row['elevation_m']\n            }\n    return station_dict\n\ndef simple_picker(tr):\n    \"\"\"Simple energy-based picker for phase picking.\"\"\"\n    data = tr.data\n    sampling_rate = tr.stats.sampling_rate\n\n    # Filter data (simple bandpass-like effect)\n    # Remove DC component\n    data = data - np.mean(data)\n\n    # Calculate short-term average and long-term average\n    window_size = int(0.1 * sampling_rate)  # 0.1 second window\n    sta_len = int(0.5 * sampling_rate)  # 0.5 seconds\n    lta_len = int(5.0 * sampling_rate)  # 5 seconds\n\n    if len(data) < lta_len * 2:\n        return []\n\n    # Calculate squared data for better peak detection\n    data_sq = data ** 2\n\n    # Calculate STA and LTA\n    sta = np.convolve(data_sq, np.ones(sta_len) / sta_len, mode='same')\n    lta = np.convolve(data_sq, np.ones(lta_len) / lta_len, mode='same')\n\n    # Avoid division by zero\n    lta = np.maximum(lta, 1e-10)\n\n    # Calculate ratio\n    ratio = sta / lta\n\n    # Find triggers with moderate threshold\n    threshold = 6.0\n    triggers = np.where(ratio > threshold)[0]\n\n    # Remove triggers that are too close together\n    min_distance = int(2.0 * sampling_rate)  # 2 seconds minimum distance between picks\n    if len(triggers) > 0:\n        filtered_triggers = [triggers[0]]\n        for trigger in triggers[1:]:\n            if trigger - filtered_triggers[-1] >= min_distance:\n                filtered_triggers.append(trigger)\n        triggers = np.array(filtered_triggers)\n\n    picks = []\n    for trigger in triggers:\n        # Find peak within a window after trigger\n        window_start = trigger\n        window_end = min(trigger + int(2.0 * sampling_rate), len(data))\n\n        if window_end > window_start:\n            window_data = data[window_start:window_end]\n            if len(window_data) > 0:\n                peak_idx = window_start + np.argmax(np.abs(window_data))\n\n                # Estimate probability based on ratio\n                prob = min(ratio[peak_idx] / 10.0, 1.0)\n\n                picks.append({\n                    'sample_idx': peak_idx,\n                    'probability': prob\n                })\n\n    return picks\n\ndef pick_phases(st):\n    \"\"\"Pick P and S waves using simple energy-based picker.\"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nPicking P and S waves using energy-based picker...\")\n\n    picks = []\n\n    # Process each trace\n    for tr in st:\n        try:\n            # Get trace metadata\n            stats = tr.stats\n            station_id = f\"{stats.network}.{stats.station}\"\n\n            # Get picks using simple picker\n            trace_picks = simple_picker(tr)\n\n            # Convert to absolute times and add to picks list\n            for pick in trace_picks:\n                time_rel = pick['sample_idx'] / stats.sampling_rate\n                pick_time = stats.starttime + time_rel\n                picks.append({\n                    'station_id': station_id,\n                    'phase_type': 'P',  # Label all as P for now\n                    'time': pick_time,\n                    'probability': pick['probability']\n                })\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Warning: Error processing trace: {e}\")\n            continue\n\n    print(f\"Found {len(picks)} phase picks\")\n    return picks\n\ndef associate_phases_gamma(picks, station_dict):\n    \"\"\"\n    Associate phase picks using a simplified GaMMA-like algorithm.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nAssociating phase picks to identify events...\")\n\n    if not picks:\n        print(\"No picks to associate\")\n        return []\n\n    # Sort picks by time\n    picks_sorted = sorted(picks, key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    # Group picks into events using time window\n    events = []\n    time_window = 30.0  # seconds - events are within this window\n\n    current_event_picks = []\n\n    for pick in picks_sorted:\n        # Check if this pick belongs to the current event\n        if not current_event_picks:\n            # First pick\n            current_event_picks = [pick]\n        else:\n            # Check time difference from first pick\n            time_diff = (pick['time'] - current_event_picks[0]['time']).total_seconds()\n\n            if time_diff <= time_window:\n                # Add to current event\n                current_event_picks.append(pick)\n            else:\n                # Time gap too large, finalize current event\n                if len(current_event_picks) >= 3:  # At least 3 picks\n                    events.append(current_event_picks)\n\n                # Start new event\n                current_event_picks = [pick]\n\n    # Don't forget the last event\n    if len(current_event_picks) >= 3:\n        events.append(current_event_picks)\n\n    # Process events to estimate event times\n    processed_events = []\n    for event_picks in events:\n        # Count unique stations\n        unique_stations = set(p['station_id'] for p in event_picks)\n\n        # Only keep events with multiple stations\n        if len(unique_stations) >= 3:\n            # Estimate event time as median of all picks\n            all_times = sorted([p['time'] for p in event_picks])\n            median_idx = len(all_times) // 2\n\n            if len(all_times) % 2 == 0:\n                event_time = all_times[median_idx - 1] + (all_times[median_idx] - all_times[median_idx - 1]) / 2\n            else:\n                event_time = all_times[median_idx]\n\n            processed_events.append({\n                'time': event_time,\n                'num_stations': len(unique_stations)\n            })\n\n    # Sort events by time\n    processed_events.sort(key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    print(f\"Identified {len(processed_events)} earthquake events\")\n    return processed_events\n\ndef associate_phases_simple(picks, station_dict):\n    \"\"\"\n    Simple association based on time clustering.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nAssociating phase picks to identify events...\")\n\n    if not picks:\n        print(\"No picks to associate\")\n        return []\n\n    # Sort picks by time\n    picks_sorted = sorted(picks, key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    # Use a sliding window to group picks\n    events = []\n    window_size = 30.0  # seconds\n    min_picks = 3  # Reduced from 5\n\n    i = 0\n    while i < len(picks_sorted):\n        # Start a new potential event\n        event_start_time = picks_sorted[i]['time']\n        event_picks = [picks_sorted[i]]\n\n        # Add picks within window\n        j = i + 1\n        while j < len(picks_sorted):\n            time_diff = (picks_sorted[j]['time'] - event_start_time).total_seconds()\n            if time_diff <= window_size:\n                event_picks.append(picks_sorted[j])\n                j += 1\n            else:\n                break\n\n        # Check if we have enough picks and stations\n        if len(event_picks) >= min_picks:\n            unique_stations = set(p['station_id'] for p in event_picks)\n\n            # Require at least 2 different stations (reduced from 3)\n            if len(unique_stations) >= 2:\n                # Estimate event time as median of earliest picks\n                event_picks.sort(key=lambda x: x['time'])\n                event_time = event_picks[len(event_picks) // 2]['time']\n\n                events.append({\n                    'time': event_time,\n                    'num_stations': len(unique_stations)\n                })\n\n        # Move to next event (skip overlapping)\n        # j is always defined because it's at least i+1\n        if j > i + 1:\n            i = j\n        else:\n            i += 1\n\n    # Sort events by time\n    events.sort(key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    print(f\"Identified {len(events)} earthquake events\")\n    return events\n\ndef write_results(events):\n    \"\"\"Write results to CSV file.\"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nWriting results to /root/results.csv...\")\n\n    results = []\n    for event in events:\n        # km/s\nVS = VP / 1.75  # km/s\nEVENT_TIME_TOLERANCE = 5.0  # seconds\n\ndef load_data():\n    \"\"\"Load waveform and station data.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Loading waveform data...\")\n    st = read('/root/data/wave.mseed')\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(st)} traces\")\n\n    print(\"Loading station data...\")\n    stations_df = pd.read_csv('/root/data/stations.csv')\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(stations_df)} station channels\")\n\n    return st, stations_df\n\ndef create_station_dict(stations_df):\n    \"\"\"Create a dictionary mapping station names to their locations.\"\"\"\n    station_dict = {}\n    for _, row in stations_df.iterrows():\n        key = f\"{row['network']}.{row['station']}\"\n        if key not in station_dict:\n            station_dict[key] = {\n                'longitude': row['longitude'],\n                'latitude': row['latitude'],\n                'elevation_m': row['elevation_m']\n            }\n    return station_dict\n\ndef simple_picker(tr):\n    \"\"\"Simple energy-based picker for phase picking.\"\"\"\n    data = tr.data\n    sampling_rate = tr.stats.sampling_rate\n\n    # Filter data (simple bandpass-like effect)\n    # Remove DC component\n    data = data - np.mean(data)\n\n    # Calculate short-term average and long-term average\n    window_size = int(0.1 * sampling_rate)  # 0.1 second window\n    sta_len = int(0.5 * sampling_rate)  # 0.5 seconds\n    lta_len = int(5.0 * sampling_rate)  # 5 seconds\n\n    if len(data) < lta_len * 2:\n        return []\n\n    # Calculate squared data for better peak detection\n    data_sq = data ** 2\n\n    # Calculate STA and LTA\n    sta = np.convolve(data_sq, np.ones(sta_len) / sta_len, mode='same')\n    lta = np.convolve(data_sq, np.ones(lta_len) / lta_len, mode='same')\n\n    # Avoid division by zero\n    lta = np.maximum(lta, 1e-10)\n\n    # Calculate ratio\n    ratio = sta / lta\n\n    # Find triggers with moderate threshold\n    threshold = 6.0\n    triggers = np.where(ratio > threshold)[0]\n\n    # Remove triggers that are too close together\n    min_distance = int(2.0 * sampling_rate)  # 2 seconds minimum distance between picks\n    if len(triggers) > 0:\n        filtered_triggers = [triggers[0]]\n        for trigger in triggers[1:]:\n            if trigger - filtered_triggers[-1] >= min_distance:\n                filtered_triggers.append(trigger)\n        triggers = np.array(filtered_triggers)\n\n    picks = []\n    for trigger in triggers:\n        # Find peak within a window after trigger\n        window_start = trigger\n        window_end = min(trigger + int(2.0 * sampling_rate), len(data))\n\n        if window_end > window_start:\n            window_data = data[window_start:window_end]\n            if len(window_data) > 0:\n                peak_idx = window_start + np.argmax(np.abs(window_data))\n\n                # Estimate probability based on ratio\n                prob = min(ratio[peak_idx] / 10.0, 1.0)\n\n                picks.append({\n                    'sample_idx': peak_idx,\n                    'probability': prob\n                })\n\n    return picks\n\ndef pick_phases(st):\n    \"\"\"Pick P and S waves using simple energy-based picker.\"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nPicking P and S waves using energy-based picker...\")\n\n    picks = []\n\n    # Process each trace\n    for tr in st:\n        try:\n            # Get trace metadata\n            stats = tr.stats\n            station_id = f\"{stats.network}.{stats.station}\"\n\n            # Get picks using simple picker\n            trace_picks = simple_picker(tr)\n\n            # Convert to absolute times and add to picks list\n            for pick in trace_picks:\n                time_rel = pick['sample_idx'] / stats.sampling_rate\n                pick_time = stats.starttime + time_rel\n                picks.append({\n                    'station_id': station_id,\n                    'phase_type': 'P',  # Label all as P for now\n                    'time': pick_time,\n                    'probability': pick['probability']\n                })\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Warning: Error processing trace: {e}\")\n            continue\n\n    print(f\"Found {len(picks)} phase picks\")\n    return picks\n\ndef associate_phases_gamma(picks, station_dict):\n    \"\"\"\n    Associate phase picks using a simplified GaMMA-like algorithm.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nAssociating phase picks to identify events...\")\n\n    if not picks:\n        print(\"No picks to associate\")\n        return []\n\n    # Sort picks by time\n    picks_sorted = sorted(picks, key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    # Group picks into events using time window\n    events = []\n    time_window = 30.0  # seconds - events are within this window\n\n    current_event_picks = []\n\n    for pick in picks_sorted:\n        # Check if this pick belongs to the current event\n        if not current_event_picks:\n            # First pick\n            current_event_picks = [pick]\n        else:\n            # Check time difference from first pick\n            time_diff = (pick['time'] - current_event_picks[0]['time']).total_seconds()\n\n            if time_diff <= time_window:\n                # Add to current event\n                current_event_picks.append(pick)\n            else:\n                # Time gap too large, finalize current event\n                if len(current_event_picks) >= 3:  # At least 3 picks\n                    events.append(current_event_picks)\n\n                # Start new event\n                current_event_picks = [pick]\n\n    # Don't forget the last event\n    if len(current_event_picks) >= 3:\n        events.append(current_event_picks)\n\n    # Process events to estimate event times\n    processed_events = []\n    for event_picks in events:\n        # Count unique stations\n        unique_stations = set(p['station_id'] for p in event_picks)\n\n        # Only keep events with multiple stations\n        if len(unique_stations) >= 3:\n            # Estimate event time as median of all picks\n            all_times = sorted([p['time'] for p in event_picks])\n            median_idx = len(all_times) // 2\n\n            if len(all_times) % 2 == 0:\n                event_time = all_times[median_idx - 1] + (all_times[median_idx] - all_times[median_idx - 1]) / 2\n            else:\n                event_time = all_times[median_idx]\n\n            processed_events.append({\n                'time': event_time,\n                'num_stations': len(unique_stations)\n            })\n\n    # Sort events by time\n    processed_events.sort(key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    print(f\"Identified {len(processed_events)} earthquake events\")\n    return processed_events\n\ndef associate_phases_simple(picks, station_dict):\n    \"\"\"\n    Simple association based on time clustering.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nAssociating phase picks to identify events...\")\n\n    if not picks:\n        print(\"No picks to associate\")\n        return []\n\n    # Sort picks by time\n    picks_sorted = sorted(picks, key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    # Use a sliding window to group picks\n    events = []\n    window_size = 30.0  # seconds\n    min_picks = 3  # Reduced from 5\n\n    i = 0\n    while i < len(picks_sorted):\n        # Start a new potential event\n        event_start_time = picks_sorted[i]['time']\n        event_picks = [picks_sorted[i]]\n\n        # Add picks within window\n        j = i + 1\n        while j < len(picks_sorted):\n            # Type check\n            if not hasattr(picks_sorted[j]['time'], 'total_seconds'):\n                print(f\"ERROR at i={i}, j={j}: picks_sorted[{j}]['time'] = {picks_sorted[j]['time']} (type: {type(picks_sorted[j]['time'])})\")\n                print(f\"  event_start_time = {event_start_time} (type: {type(event_start_time)})\")\n                print(f\"  picks_sorted[{j}] = {picks_sorted[j]}\")\n                break\n            time_diff = (picks_sorted[j]['time'] - event_start_time).total_seconds()\n            if time_diff <= window_size:\n                event_picks.append(picks_sorted[j])\n                j += 1\n            else:\n                break\n\n        # Check if we have enough picks and stations\n        if len(event_picks) >= min_picks:\n            unique_stations = set(p['station_id'] for p in event_picks)\n\n            # Require at least 2 different stations (reduced from 3)\n            if len(unique_stations) >= 2:\n                #
